--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- YUNO SCRIPT KEY SYSTEM (PLATOBOOST INTEGRATION)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
local SERVICE_ID = 30793
local HMAC_KEY = "c5f0932b-3cdb-4dca-b2a3-5bdc958bca23"
local KEY_LINK = "https://gateway.platoboost.com/a?id=30793"
local KEY_FILE = "yuno_key.txt"

local function getYunoKey()
    if type(key) == "string" and key ~= "" then return key end
    if type(getgenv().key) == "string" and getgenv().key ~= "" then return getgenv().key end
    if type(_G.key) == "string" and _G.key ~= "" then return _G.key end
    if type(getgenv().YunoKey) == "string" and getgenv().YunoKey ~= "" then return getgenv().YunoKey end
    if isfile and isfile(KEY_FILE) then
        local ok, saved = pcall(readfile, KEY_FILE)
        if ok and type(saved) == "string" and saved ~= "" then
            local cleanKey = saved:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            if cleanKey and #cleanKey > 0 then
                return cleanKey
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function getHWID()
    local hwid = nil
    pcall(function()
        if gethwid then
            hwid = gethwid()
        elseif get_hwid then
            hwid = get_hwid()
        else
            hwid = game:GetService("RbxAnalyticsService"):GetClientId()
        end
    end)
    return hwid or "UNKNOWN_HWID"
end

local function httpRequest(options)
    local req = (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request) or request or http_request
    if req then
        return req(options)
    elseif game and game.HttpGet then
        local ok, body = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, options.Url)
        if ok then
            return { StatusCode = 200, Body = body }
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function verifyPlatoboostKey(userKey)
    if not userKey or userKey == "" then return false, "No key provided." end
    local hwid = getHWID()
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
    
    local endpoints = {
        "https://api.platoboost.com/public/whitelist/" .. tostring(SERVICE_ID) .. "/" .. hwid .. "?key=" .. userKey,
        "https://api.platoboost.net/public/whitelist/" .. tostring(SERVICE_ID) .. "/" .. hwid .. "?key=" .. userKey,
        "https://api.platoboost.app/v1/public/whitelist/" .. tostring(SERVICE_ID) .. "?key=" .. userKey .. "&hwid=" .. hwid,
        "https://api.platoboost.com/public/whitelist/" .. HMAC_KEY .. "/" .. hwid .. "?key=" .. userKey,
        "https://dash.platoboost.app/api1?id=" .. tostring(SERVICE_ID) .. "&key=" .. userKey .. "&hwid=" .. hwid .. "&public_key=" .. HMAC_KEY
    }
    
    for _, url in ipairs(endpoints) do
        local success, res = pcall(function()
            return httpRequest({ Url = url, Method = "GET" })
        end)
        
        if success and res and (res.StatusCode == 200 or res.Status == 200) and res.Body then
            local decodeSuccess, data = pcall(function()
                return HttpService:JSONDecode(res.Body)
            end)
            if decodeSuccess and type(data) == "table" then
                if data.valid == true or data.success == true or data.whitelisted == true or data.status == "success" or data.status == 200 then
                    return true, "Key validated successfully!"
                elseif data.message then
                    return false, tostring(data.message)
                end
            elseif res.Body:lower():find("valid") or res.Body:lower():find("true") or res.Body:lower():find("success") then
                if not res.Body:lower():find("invalid") and not res.Body:lower():find("false") then
                    return true, "Key validated successfully!"
                end
            end
        end
    end
    
    return false, "Invalid or unverified key."
end

local providedKey = getYunoKey()
local isValid, verifyMsg = verifyPlatoboostKey(providedKey)

if not isValid then
    if setclipboard then
        pcall(setclipboard, KEY_LINK)
    end
    local msg = "[Yuno Key System]: Access Denied! " .. tostring(verifyMsg or "Invalid key")
    warn(msg)
    warn("[Yuno Key System]: Please get your key from " .. KEY_LINK .. " (Link copied to clipboard!)")
    warn("[Yuno Key System]: Usage: key = "YOUR_KEY" loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/notthatzurrz-cmyk/yuno/refs/heads/main/yuno%201.txt"))()")
    pcall(function()
        game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
            Title = "Yuno Key System",
            Text = "Invalid Key! Key link copied to clipboard.",
            Duration = 7
        })
    end)
    return
else
    if writefile and providedKey then
        pcall(writefile, KEY_FILE, providedKey)
    end
    print("[Yuno Key System]: Key Verified! Loading Yuno Script...")
end

local function ValidTraceback(s)

local dotPos = string.find(s, "%.")

local colonPos = string.find(s, ":")

if not dotPos then

return false

end

if not colonPos then

return true

end

return dotPos < colonPos

end

local function TracebackLines(str, lvl)

local pos = lvl

return function()

if not pos then

return nil

end

local p1, p2 = string.find(str, "\r?\n", pos)

local line

if p1 then

line = str:sub(pos, p1 - 1)

pos = p2 + 1

else

line = str:sub(pos)

pos = nil

end

return line

end

end

if not ValidTraceback(Line) then continue end

return table.concat(NewTraceback, "\n") .. "\n"

setstackhidden = setstackhidden or function(fn_or_level, hidden)

assert(typeof(hidden) == "boolean", "hidden must be boolean")

local ok, fn = pcall(function()

if typeof(fn_or_level) == "number" then

return debug.info(fn_or_level + 2, "f")

end

return fn_or_level

end)

assert(ok and fn, "invalid argument #1 to 'setstackhidden'")

hidden_fn[fn] = not hidden

end

local TrustedFunctions = setmetatable({}, {

__mode = "k"

})

local function TrustFunction(fn)

if type(fn) == "function" then

TrustedFunctions[fn] = true

end

return fn

end

local function IsTrustedFunction(fn)

return TrustedFunctions[fn] == true

end


local SpoofedFunctions = setmetatable({}, {

__mode = "k"

})

local SafeHook = function(hookfn, ...)

local args = { ... }

local func, inst, metamethod, detour

if hookfn == hookmetamethod then

inst = args[1]

metamethod = args[2]

detour = args[3]

else

func = args[1]

detour = args[2]

end

if not detour then return nil end

if hookfn == hookfunction and func and iscclosure(func) then

detour = newcclosure(detour)

end

if not iscclosure(detour) then

detour = newcclosure(detour)

end

pcall(setstackhidden, detour, true)

local original_func = nil

local ok, err = pcall(function()

TrustFunction(detour)

if hookfn == hookmetamethod then

original_func = hookfn(inst, metamethod, detour)

else

original_func = hookfn(func, detour)

end

end)

if ok then

SpoofedFunctions[detour] = true

if original_func ~= nil then

SpoofedFunctions[original_func] = true

end

else

warn("[Yuno]: SafeHook failed: " .. tostring(err))

end

return original_func

end

local SafeCall

if getthreadidentity and setthreadidentity then

SafeCall = function(func, ...)

if checkcaller() then

return func(...)

end

local old = getthreadidentity()

if old ~= 2 then

setthreadidentity(2)

end

local results = table.pack(func(...))

if old ~= 2 then

setthreadidentity(old)

end

return table.unpack(results, 1, results.n)

end

else

SafeCall = function(func, ...)

return func(...)

end

end

if type(fn) == "function" and SpoofedFunctions[fn] then

return false

end

return old_isfh(fn)

end)

end)

end

local function installNameMask()

if not (hookmetamethod and getcallingscript) then return end

if getgenv().__YunoNameMaskInstalled then return end

getgenv().__YunoNameMaskInstalled = true

local detectedCallerNames = {

["localscript3"] = true,

["miscellaneouscontroller"] = true,

}

local callerVerdicts = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local giOriginal

giOriginal = SafeHook(hookmetamethod, game, "__index", function(self, key)

if antidetectEnabled and (key == "Name" or key == "Text") then

local okCaller, caller = pcall(getcallingscript)

if okCaller and caller ~= nil then

local blocked = callerVerdicts[caller]

if blocked == nil then

local okVerdict, result = pcall(function()

local n = giOriginal(caller, "Name")

return detectedCallerNames[string.lower(tostring(n))] == true

end)

blocked = (okVerdict and result) or false

callerVerdicts[caller] = blocked

end

if blocked then

return ""

end

end

end

return giOriginal(self, key)

end)

end

local function BuildSubTable()

local num_empty = math.random(1, 5)

local empty_map = {}

local empty_slots = { 7 }

empty_map[7] = true

while #empty_slots < num_empty do

local slot = math.random(1, 6)

if not empty_map[slot] then

empty_map[slot] = true

table.insert(empty_slots, slot)

end

end

table.sort(empty_slots)

local result = {}

for i = 1, 7 do

if empty_map[i] then

result[i] = {}

else

result[i] = kFilledSub

end

end

return result, empty_slots

end

local function ApplyTransforms(t, mask, empty_slots)

local payload = t[1]

local outer_index = #payload

local inner_index = empty_slots[math.random(1, #empty_slots)]

local derived

local outer_val = payload[outer_index]

if type(outer_val) == "table" and type(inner_index) == "number" then

derived = outer_val[inner_index]

else

for i = outer_index, 1, -1 do

if type(payload[i]) ~= "table" then continue end

local candidate = payload[i]

if type(inner_index) == "number" and candidate[inner_index] ~= nil then

derived = candidate[inner_index]

break

else

derived = candidate

break

end

end

if derived == nil then

derived = {}

end

end

local written = {}

local kSlotMapRef = kSlotMap

for _, value in ipairs(mask) do

local slot = kSlotMapRef[value]

if slot and not written[slot] then

t[slot] = derived

written[slot] = true

end

end

return t

end

local function BuildPayload(challenge, mask)

local sub_table, empty_slots = BuildSubTable()

local total_idx = math.random(1, 8)

local payload = { client_id, buffer.tostring(challenge) }

local extra_strings = math.random(0, 2)

for _ = 1, extra_strings do

payload[#payload + 1] = ""

end

while #payload < (total_idx - 1) do

payload[#payload + 1] = math.random(5, 100000)

end

payload[#payload + 1] = sub_table

local t = {

payload,

{},

nil,

nil,

nil,

nil,

nil

}

return ApplyTransforms(t, mask, empty_slots)

end

task.spawn(function()

pcall(function()

getfenv().script = ac_script

end)

local hijackDeadline = tick() + 20

while not hijack_ready and tick() < hijackDeadline do

task.wait()

end

if not hijack_ready then

warn("[Yuno]: challenge hook never captured first packet, leaving anticheat script enabled")

return

end

if ac_script then

ac_script.Enabled = false

end

ac_event.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)

last = tick()

local remote = Instance.new("RemoteEvent")

pcall(function() remote:FireServer() end)

local t = { ... }

local challenge = t[1]

local index = t[2]

local mask = t[3]

if typeof(challenge) ~= "buffer" or type(index) ~= "number" or type(mask) ~= "table" then

LocalPlayer:Kick("[Yuno]: Bypass failed! n3")

return

end

local okPayload, payload = pcall(BuildPayload, challenge, mask)

if not okPayload then return end

task.defer(function()

local since_last = tick() - (last or 0)

local desired_wait = expected_interval - since_last

if desired_wait > 0 then

task.wait(desired_wait)

end

pcall(function()

ac_event:FireServer(table.unpack(payload, 1, 5))

end)

last = tick()

pcall(function() remote:Destroy() end)

end)

end)

bypassed = true

pcall(function() monitor_conn:Disconnect() end)

end)


local function installNamecallGuard()

if not (hookmetamethod and getnamecallmethod) then return end

if getgenv().__YunoNamecallGuardInstalled then return end

getgenv().__YunoNamecallGuardInstalled = true

local nc_original

nc_original = SafeHook(hookmetamethod, game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)

local method = getnamecallmethod()

if (method == "Kick" or method == "kick") and self == Players.LocalPlayer and not checkcaller() then

return nil

end

return nc_original(self, ...)

end)

if not nc_original then

warn("[Yuno]: __namecall guard unavailable, relying on Kick function hooks")

end

end

installNamecallGuard()

for _, name in ipairs(kKickNames) do

pcall(function()

local func = LocalPlayer[name]

if type(func) ~= "function" then return end

local oldfunc

oldfunc = SafeHook(hookfunction, func, function(self, ...)

if self == LocalPlayer and not checkcaller() then

return nil

end

return oldfunc(self, ...)

end)

if not oldfunc then

LocalPlayer:Kick("[Yuno]: Bypass failed! n1")

end

end)

end

local function installErrorKillers()

pcall(function()

if not getconnections then return end

for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(ScriptContext.Error)) do

if not conn.Function then continue end

if IsTrustedFunction(conn.Function) then continue end

SafeHook(hookfunction, conn.Function, function()

return nil

end)

end

end)

pcall(function()

SafeHook(hookfunction, ScriptContext.Error.Connect, function(...)

return nil

end)

end)

end





task.spawn(function()

local t0 = tick()

while tick() - t0 < 240 do

local ch = LocalPlayer.Character

if ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then break end

task.wait(0.25)

end

task.wait(1)

pcall(installControllerNeutralizers)

pcall(installNameMask)

pcall(installErrorKillers)

end)

local bypassDeadline = tick() + 30

while not bypassed and tick() < bypassDeadline do

task.wait(0.5)

end

if not bypassed then

warn("[Yuno]: AC bypass timed out, continuing anyway")

end

task.wait(1)

end))

end

do
    -- 1. Ensure game client is completely loaded into data model
    if not game:IsLoaded() then
        pcall(function() game.Loaded:Wait() end)
    end

    local plrs = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
    local lp = plrs.LocalPlayer
    if not lp then
        local t0 = os.clock()
        repeat
            task.wait(0.1)
            lp = plrs.LocalPlayer
        until lp or (os.clock() - t0 > 30)
    end

    local repStorage = cloneref(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"))
    local playerGui = lp and (lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 30))
    local playerScripts = lp and (lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts") or lp:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 30))

    -- Wait for critical replicated modules
    pcall(function() repStorage:WaitForChild("Modules", 30) end)

    -- Dynamic controller scanner to prevent early require crashes and engine deadlocks
    local function areControllersReady()
        if not lp then return false end
        local ps = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
        if not ps then return false end
        local ctrl = ps:FindFirstChild("Controllers")
        if not ctrl then return false end
        
        local fc = ctrl:FindFirstChild("FighterController")
        local cc = ctrl:FindFirstChild("CameraController")
        if not (fc and cc and fc:IsA("ModuleScript") and cc:IsA("ModuleScript")) then
            return false
        end

        -- Safe require probe to verify internal module tables are fully built
        local okFC, modFC = pcall(require, fc)
        if not okFC or type(modFC) ~= "table" then
            return false
        end
        return true
    end

    -- Check match stage / UI readiness
    local function isMatchOrScreenReady()
        if not repStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules") then return false end
        local pg = lp and lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if not pg then return false end

        -- Loading screen / black transition overlay check
        local loadingGui = pg:FindFirstChild("LoadingGui") or pg:FindFirstChild("TeleportGui") or pg:FindFirstChild("BlackScreen") or pg:FindFirstChild("LoadingScreen")
        if loadingGui and (loadingGui.Enabled == true) then
            return false
        end

        local matchUI = (
            pg:FindFirstChild("MapVoting") or
            pg:FindFirstChild("MainGui") or
            pg:FindFirstChild("MatchGui") or
            pg:FindFirstChild("HUD") or
            pg:FindFirstChild("VotingGui") or
            pg:FindFirstChild("GunSelection") or
            pg:FindFirstChild("LoadoutGui") or
            pg:FindFirstChild("ClassSelection") or
            pg:FindFirstChild("RoundGui")
        )

        local hasChar = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local controllersOk = areControllersReady()

        return (controllersOk and (matchUI ~= nil or hasChar ~= nil))
    end

    local waitStart = os.clock()
    while (os.clock() - waitStart < 35) and not isMatchOrScreenReady() do
        task.wait(0.25)
    end

    -- Essential settlement buffer to ensure all C++ engine replication and singletons are stable
    task.wait(1.2)
end

do
local AntiAfkPlayers = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
local AntiAfkLocal = AntiAfkPlayers.LocalPlayer
local antiAfkToken = {}
local antiAfkSlot = getgenv().__YunoAntiAfk
if type(antiAfkSlot) == "table" and antiAfkSlot.Connection then
pcall(function() antiAfkSlot.Connection:Disconnect() end)
end

local function antiAfkNudge()
pcall(function()
local vim = Instance.new("VirtualInputManager")
vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.F15, false, game)
vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.F15, false, game)
vim:Destroy()
end)
end

if AntiAfkLocal then
local antiAfkConn = AntiAfkLocal.Idled:Connect(antiAfkNudge)
getgenv().__YunoAntiAfk = {
Token = antiAfkToken,
Connection = antiAfkConn,
}
task.spawn(function()
while true do
task.wait(300)
local live = getgenv().__YunoAntiAfk
if type(live) ~= "table" or live.Token ~= antiAfkToken then
return
end
antiAfkNudge()
end
end)
end
end

do
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local SkinPlayers = game:GetService("Players")

local skinState = {equipped={}, favorites={}, hooked=false, originals={}, mods={}, viewingProfile=nil, saveFile="yuno_skins_v2.json", constructingWeapon=nil}

local SUPPORTED_TYPES = {
Skin = true,
Charm = true,
Wrap = true,
Wrapping = true,
Dance = true,
Emote = true,
}

local function isSupportedCosmetic(data, name)
if not data then return false end
if type(name) == "string" and string.find(name, "MISSING_", 1, true) then return false end
local t = data.Type
if t == "Finisher" or t == "KillEffect" or t == "CallingCard" then return false end
if SUPPORTED_TYPES[t] then return true end
if type(name) == "string" then
local n = name:lower()
if n:find("charm", 1, true) or n:find("wrap", 1, true)
or n:find("dance", 1, true) or n:find("emote", 1, true) then
return true
end
end
return false
end

local function scClone(name, typ, opts)
local lib = skinState.mods.CosmeticLibrary
if not lib or not lib.Cosmetics then return nil end
local base = lib.Cosmetics[name]
if not base then return nil end
local d = {}
for k, v in pairs(base) do d[k] = v end
d.Name = name
d.Type = d.Type or typ
d.Seed = d.Seed or math.random(1, 1000000)
local el = skinState.mods.EnumLibrary
if el then
local ok, eid = pcall(function() return el:ToEnum(name) end)
if ok and eid then d.Enum, d.ObjectID = eid, d.ObjectID or eid end
end
if opts then
if opts.inverted ~= nil then d.Inverted = opts.inverted end
if opts.favoritesOnly ~= nil then d.OnlyUseFavorites = opts.favoritesOnly end
end
return d
end

local function scSave()
if not writefile or not makefolder then return end
pcall(function()
local cfg = {equipped={}, favorites=skinState.favorites}
for w, cs in pairs(skinState.equipped) do
cfg.equipped[w] = {}
for ct, cd in pairs(cs) do
if cd and cd.Name then
cfg.equipped[w][ct] = {name=cd.Name, seed=cd.Seed, inverted=cd.Inverted}
end
end
end
makefolder("unlockall")
writefile(skinState.saveFile, HttpService:JSONEncode(cfg))
end)
end

local function scLoad()
if not readfile or not isfile or not isfile(skinState.saveFile) then return end
pcall(function()
local cfg = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(skinState.saveFile))
if cfg.equipped then
for w, cs in pairs(cfg.equipped) do
skinState.equipped[w] = {}
for ct, cd in pairs(cs) do
local cl = scClone(cd.name, ct, {inverted=cd.inverted})
if cl then cl.Seed = cd.seed skinState.equipped[w][ct] = cl end
end
end
end
skinState.favorites = cfg.favorites or {}
end)
end

local function scRefreshHeldWeapon(wname)
    pcall(function()
        local p = SkinPlayers.LocalPlayer
        if not p then return end
        local ps = p:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
        local ctrl = ps and (ps:FindFirstChild("Controllers") or ps:FindFirstChild("Modules"))
        local fc = ctrl and (ctrl:FindFirstChild("ClientFighterController", true) or ctrl:FindFirstChild("FighterController", true))
        local lf = nil
        if fc then
            local ok, res = pcall(require, fc)
            if ok and res then lf = res.LocalFighter end
        end
        if lf then
            local itemsToUpdate = {}
            if lf.EquippedItem then table.insert(itemsToUpdate, lf.EquippedItem) end
            if type(lf.Items) == "table" then
                for _, it in ipairs(lf.Items) do
                    if it ~= lf.EquippedItem then table.insert(itemsToUpdate, it) end
                end
            end
            if type(lf._client_items) == "table" then
                for _, it in pairs(lf._client_items) do
                    if type(it) == "table" and it.Name and not table.find(itemsToUpdate, it) then
                        table.insert(itemsToUpdate, it)
                    end
                end
            end

            for _, item in ipairs(itemsToUpdate) do
                if not wname or item.Name == wname or wname == "Dances" then
                    local eq = skinState.equipped[item.Name]
                    if item.Data then
                        if eq then
                            for ct, cd in pairs(eq) do
                                item.Data[ct] = cd
                            end
                            if eq.Skin then
                                item.Data.Skin = eq.Skin
                                item.Data.Name = eq.Skin.Name
                            end
                        else
                            item.Data.Skin = nil
                            item.Data.Wrap = nil
                            item.Data.Charm = nil
                            item.Data.Name = item.Name
                        end
                    end
                    if item == lf.EquippedItem then
                        if item._DestroyViewModel and item._CreateViewModel then
                            pcall(function()
                                item:_DestroyViewModel()
                                item:_CreateViewModel(item.Data)
                            end)
                        elseif item.ViewModel and item.ViewModel._UpdateWrap then
                            pcall(function() item.ViewModel:_UpdateWrap() end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function scEquip(wname, ctype, ckey, opts)
    if not skinState.hooked then scUnlockAll() end
    if ctype == "Dance" or ctype == "Emote" then wname = "Dances" end
    if not ckey or ckey == "" or ckey == "None" then
        if skinState.equipped[wname] then
            skinState.equipped[wname][ctype] = nil
            if not next(skinState.equipped[wname]) then skinState.equipped[wname] = nil end
        end
    else
        local cl = scClone(ckey, ctype, opts)
        if cl then
            skinState.equipped[wname] = skinState.equipped[wname] or {}
            skinState.equipped[wname][ctype] = cl
        end
    end
    task.defer(function()
        pcall(function()
            if skinState.mods.DataController then
                skinState.mods.DataController.CurrentData:Replicate("WeaponInventory")
            end
        end)
        scSave()
        scRefreshHeldWeapon(wname)
    end)
end

function scUnlockAll()
if skinState.hooked then return true end
local p = SkinPlayers.LocalPlayer
if not p then return false end
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ps = p:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts") or p:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 15)
if not ps then return false end
local ctrl = ps:FindFirstChild("Controllers") or ps:WaitForChild("Controllers", 15)
if not ctrl then return false end
local modsFolder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules", true) or ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
local clMod = (modsFolder and modsFolder:FindFirstChild("CosmeticLibrary")) or ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("CosmeticLibrary", true)
local ilMod = (modsFolder and modsFolder:FindFirstChild("ItemLibrary")) or ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("ItemLibrary", true)
local elMod = (modsFolder and modsFolder:FindFirstChild("EnumLibrary")) or ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("EnumLibrary", true)
local dcMod = ctrl:FindFirstChild("PlayerDataController", true) or ctrl:FindFirstChild("PlayerDataController")

local elOk, el = pcall(require, elMod)
local clOk, cl = pcall(require, clMod)
local ilOk, il = pcall(require, ilMod)
local dcOk, dc = pcall(require, dcMod)
if not (clOk and cl and cl.Cosmetics and dcOk and dc) then return false end
skinState.mods = {EnumLibrary=(elOk and el or nil), CosmeticLibrary=cl, ItemLibrary=(ilOk and il or nil), DataController=dc}
skinState.hooked = true

skinState.originals = {
OwnsCosmetic = cl.OwnsCosmetic,
OwnsCosmeticNormally = cl.OwnsCosmeticNormally,
OwnsCosmeticUniversally = cl.OwnsCosmeticUniversally,
OwnsCosmeticForWeapon = cl.OwnsCosmeticForWeapon,
DCGet = dc.Get,
DCGetWeaponData = dc.GetWeaponData,
}

local function spoofOrOriginal(origFn)
return function(self, inv, name, wep)
if type(name) == "string" and not string.find(name, "MISSING_", 1, true) then
if isSupportedCosmetic(cl.Cosmetics[name], name) then return true end
end
if origFn then return origFn(self, inv, name, wep) end
return false
end
end
cl.OwnsCosmetic = spoofOrOriginal(skinState.originals.OwnsCosmetic)
if skinState.originals.OwnsCosmeticNormally then
cl.OwnsCosmeticNormally = spoofOrOriginal(skinState.originals.OwnsCosmeticNormally)
end
if skinState.originals.OwnsCosmeticUniversally then
cl.OwnsCosmeticUniversally = spoofOrOriginal(skinState.originals.OwnsCosmeticUniversally)
end
if skinState.originals.OwnsCosmeticForWeapon then
cl.OwnsCosmeticForWeapon = spoofOrOriginal(skinState.originals.OwnsCosmeticForWeapon)
end

dc.Get = function(self, key)
if key == "CosmeticInventory" then
local okReal, realData = pcall(skinState.originals.DCGet, self, key)
local proxy = {}
if okReal and type(realData) == "table" then
for k, v in pairs(realData) do
if isSupportedCosmetic(cl.Cosmetics[k], k) then proxy[k] = v end
end
end
return setmetatable(proxy, {
__index = function(_, k)
if type(k) == "string" and isSupportedCosmetic(cl.Cosmetics[k], k) then return true end
return nil
end
})
end
if key == "FavoritedCosmetics" then
local okFav, data = pcall(skinState.originals.DCGet, self, key)
local r = (okFav and type(data) == "table") and table.clone(data) or {}
for w, favs in pairs(skinState.favorites) do
r[w] = r[w] or {}
for n, isF in pairs(favs) do r[w][n] = isF end
end
return r
end
return skinState.originals.DCGet(self, key)
end

dc.GetWeaponData = function(self, wname)
local okW, data = pcall(skinState.originals.DCGetWeaponData, self, wname)
if not okW or not data then return data end
local m = {}
for k, v in pairs(data) do m[k] = v end
m.Name = wname
local eq = skinState.equipped[wname]
if eq then
for ct, cd in pairs(eq) do m[ct] = cd end
end
return m
end

if hookmetamethod then
local rem = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
local dr = rem and rem:FindFirstChild("Data")
local eqR = dr and dr:FindFirstChild("EquipCosmetic")
local fvR = dr and dr:FindFirstChild("FavoriteCosmetic")
if eqR then
local oldNC
pcall(function()
oldNC = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
if not skinState.hooked then return oldNC(self, ...) end
if getnamecallmethod() ~= "FireServer" then return oldNC(self, ...) end
local a = {...}
if self == eqR then
local wn, ct, cn = a[1], a[2], a[3]
local opts = a[4] or {}
if type(ct) ~= "string" then return oldNC(self, ...) end
local hasName = cn and cn ~= "None" and cn ~= ""
if hasName then
local okReal, realInv = pcall(skinState.originals.DCGet, dc, "CosmeticInventory")
if okReal and type(realInv) == "table" and rawget(realInv, cn) ~= nil then
return oldNC(self, ...)
end
if not isSupportedCosmetic(cl.Cosmetics[cn], cn) then
return oldNC(self, ...)
end
end
local target = wn
if ct == "Dance" or ct == "Emote" then target = "Dances" end
skinState.equipped[target] = skinState.equipped[target] or {}
if not hasName then
skinState.equipped[target][ct] = nil
if not next(skinState.equipped[target]) then skinState.equipped[target] = nil end
else
local cloned = scClone(cn, ct, {inverted=opts.IsInverted, favoritesOnly=opts.OnlyUseFavorites})
if cloned then skinState.equipped[target][ct] = cloned end
end
task.defer(function()
pcall(function() dc.CurrentData:Replicate("WeaponInventory") end)
task.wait(0.2)
scSave()
end)
return
end
if self == fvR then
local n2 = a[2]
if isSupportedCosmetic(cl.Cosmetics[n2], n2) then
skinState.favorites[a[1]] = skinState.favorites[a[1]] or {}
skinState.favorites[a[1]][n2] = a[3] or nil
scSave()
task.spawn(function()
pcall(function() dc.CurrentData:Replicate("FavoritedCosmetics") end)
end)
return
end
return oldNC(self, ...)
end
return oldNC(self, ...)
end)
end)
end
end

local ciOk, CI = pcall(require, ps.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem)
if ciOk and CI and CI._CreateViewModel then
    local oCVM = CI._CreateViewModel
    CI._CreateViewModel = function(self, ref)
        local wp = self.ClientFighter and self.ClientFighter.Player
        local isLocal = (wp == p)
        skinState.constructingWeapon = isLocal and self.Name or nil
        local eq = isLocal and skinState.equipped[self.Name]
        if eq then
            if self.Data then
                for ct, cd in pairs(eq) do self.Data[ct] = cd end
                if eq.Skin then
                    self.Data.Skin = eq.Skin
                    self.Data.Name = eq.Skin.Name
                end
            end
            if ref then
                local okDk, dk = pcall(function() return self:ToEnum("Data") end)
                if okDk and dk and ref[dk] then
                    for ct, cd in pairs(eq) do
                        local okK, ck = pcall(function() return self:ToEnum(ct) end)
                        if okK and ck then ref[dk][ck] = cd end
                    end
                    if eq.Skin then
                        local okN, nk = pcall(function() return self:ToEnum("Name") end)
                        if okN and nk then ref[dk][nk] = eq.Skin.Name end
                    end
                elseif ref.Data then
                    for ct, cd in pairs(eq) do ref.Data[ct] = cd end
                    if eq.Skin then ref.Data.Name = eq.Skin.Name end
                end
            end
        end
        local result = oCVM(self, ref or self.Data)
        skinState.constructingWeapon = nil
        return result
    end
end

if ilOk and il and il.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData then
local oGVMI = il.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData
il.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData = function(self, wd, hr)
if not wd then return oGVMI(self, wd, hr) end
local wn = wd.Name
local show = (wd.Skin and skinState.equipped[wn] and wd.Skin == skinState.equipped[wn].Skin)
or (skinState.viewingProfile == p and skinState.equipped[wn] and skinState.equipped[wn].Skin)
if show and skinState.equipped[wn] and skinState.equipped[wn].Skin then
local si = self.ViewModels and self.ViewModels[skinState.equipped[wn].Skin.Name]
if si then return si[hr and "ImageHighResolution" or "Image"] or si.Image end
end
return oGVMI(self, wd, hr)
end
end

local crc = ps.Modules and ps.Modules:FindFirstChild("ClientReplicatedClasses")
local cf = crc and crc:FindFirstChild("ClientFighter")
local cvmModule = cf and cf:FindFirstChild("ClientViewModel")
if cvmModule then
local cvmOk, CVM = pcall(require, cvmModule)
if cvmOk and CVM then
if CVM.GetCharm then
local oGC = CVM.GetCharm
CVM.GetCharm = function(self)
local ci = self.ClientItem
local wp = ci and ci.ClientFighter and ci.ClientFighter.Player
local wn = ci and ci.Name
if wn and wp == p and skinState.equipped[wn] and skinState.equipped[wn].Charm then
return skinState.equipped[wn].Charm
end
return oGC(self)
end
end
if CVM.GetWrap then
local oGW = CVM.GetWrap
CVM.GetWrap = function(self)
local ci = self.ClientItem
local wp = ci and ci.ClientFighter and ci.ClientFighter.Player
local wn = ci and ci.Name
if wn and wp == p and skinState.equipped[wn] and skinState.equipped[wn].Wrap then
return skinState.equipped[wn].Wrap
end
return oGW(self)
end
end
local oNew = CVM.new
CVM.new = function(rd, ci)
local wp = ci and ci.ClientFighter and ci.ClientFighter.Player
local wn = (wp == p) and (skinState.constructingWeapon or ci and ci.Name) or nil
if wp == p and wn and skinState.equipped[wn] then
local rcOk, RC = pcall(require, ReplicatedStorage.Modules.ReplicatedClass)
if rcOk and RC then
local okDk, dk = pcall(function() return RC:ToEnum("Data") end)
if okDk and dk then
rd[dk] = rd[dk] or {}
for ct, cd in pairs(skinState.equipped[wn]) do
local okK, ck = pcall(function() return RC:ToEnum(ct) end)
if okK and ck then rd[dk][ck] = cd end
end
end
end
end
local result = oNew(rd, ci)
if wp == p and wn and skinState.equipped[wn] and skinState.equipped[wn].Wrap
and result and result._UpdateWrap then
pcall(function() result:_UpdateWrap() end)
task.delay(0.1, function()
if not result._destroyed then pcall(function() result:_UpdateWrap() end) end
end)
end
return result
end
end
end

local ecOk, EC = pcall(require, ctrl:WaitForChild("EmoteController", 10))
if ecOk and EC and EC.GetEmotes then
local oGE = EC.GetEmotes
EC.GetEmotes = function(self)
local okE, em = pcall(oGE, self)
if not okE or type(em) ~= "table" then em = em or {} end
for n, c in pairs(cl.Cosmetics) do
if isSupportedCosmetic(c, n) and (c.Type == "Dance" or c.Type == "Emote"
or (type(n) == "string" and (n:lower():find("dance", 1, true) or n:lower():find("emote", 1, true)))) then
if not em[n] then em[n] = c end
end
end
return em
end
end

pcall(function()
local VP = require(p.PlayerScripts.Modules.Pages.ViewProfile)
if VP and VP.Fetch then
local oF = VP.Fetch
VP.Fetch = function(self, tp)
skinState.viewingProfile = tp
return oF(self, tp)
end
end
end)

scLoad()
return true
end

function scLockAll()
if not skinState.hooked then return end
skinState.hooked = false
local cl = skinState.mods.CosmeticLibrary
local dc = skinState.mods.DataController
if cl then
pcall(function() cl.OwnsCosmetic = skinState.originals.OwnsCosmetic end)
pcall(function() cl.OwnsCosmeticNormally = skinState.originals.OwnsCosmeticNormally end)
pcall(function() cl.OwnsCosmeticUniversally = skinState.originals.OwnsCosmeticUniversally end)
pcall(function() cl.OwnsCosmeticForWeapon = skinState.originals.OwnsCosmeticForWeapon end)
end
if dc then
pcall(function() dc.Get = skinState.originals.DCGet end)
pcall(function() dc.GetWeaponData = skinState.originals.DCGetWeaponData end)
end
for k in pairs(skinState.equipped) do skinState.equipped[k] = nil end
for k in pairs(skinState.favorites) do skinState.favorites[k] = nil end
end

if not getgenv().__YunoSkinsAuto then
getgenv().__YunoSkinsAuto = true
task.spawn(function()
local p = SkinPlayers.LocalPlayer
if not p then return end
local t0 = tick()
while tick() - t0 < 300 do
local ch = p.Character
if ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then break end
task.wait(0.5)
end
task.wait(1)
for attempt = 1, 4 do
if scUnlockAll() then
print("[Yuno] skin changer active (" .. attempt .. ")")
return
end
task.wait(3)
end
warn("[Yuno] skin changer could not find game modules")
end)
end

local function yunoToastLoader()
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local guiParent = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui")
local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
gui.Name = "YunoSplashHUD"
gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
gui.DisplayOrder = 696769676967
gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
gui.Parent = guiParent
local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
label.Name = "LoadingLabel"
label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
label.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(280, 48)
label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
label.TextSize = 22
label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 255)
label.Text = "loading."
label.Parent = gui
local accum = 0
local dotIndex = 1
local conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
accum = accum + dt
if accum < 0.35 then return end
accum = 0
dotIndex = dotIndex % 3 + 1
label.Text = "loading" .. string.rep(".", dotIndex)
end)
return function()
if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
if gui then gui:Destroy() end
end
end

getgenv().YunoModuleVault = getgenv().YunoModuleVault or {}

local function yunoFetchModule(moduleRef, timeoutSec)
    if not moduleRef then return {} end
    local cache = getgenv().YunoModuleVault
    local key = typeof(moduleRef) == "Instance" and moduleRef:GetFullName() or tostring(moduleRef)
    if cache[key] ~= nil then return cache[key] end
    local deadline = os.clock() + (timeoutSec or 10)
    local lastErr = nil
    while os.clock() < deadline do
        if typeof(moduleRef) == "Instance" and not moduleRef.Parent then
            task.wait(0.1)
        else
            local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleRef)
            if ok and result ~= nil then
                cache[key] = result
                return result
            end
            lastErr = result
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end
    warn("[Yuno] module load fallback for: " .. key .. " (" .. tostring(lastErr) .. ")")
    local dummy = {}
    cache[key] = dummy
    return dummy
end

getgenv().YunoRequireModule = yunoFetchModule

local function yunoWarmupModules()
local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local players = game:GetService("Players")
local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer or players.PlayerAdded:Wait()
local playerScripts = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 8)
local modulesFolder = replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules", 8)
task.spawn(function() pcall(function() yunoFetchModule(modulesFolder:WaitForChild("EnumLibrary", 4)) end) end)
task.spawn(function() pcall(function() yunoFetchModule(modulesFolder:WaitForChild("Utility", 4)) end) end)
local controllers = playerScripts:WaitForChild("Controllers", 8)
task.spawn(function() pcall(function() yunoFetchModule(controllers:WaitForChild("FighterController", 4)) end) end)
task.spawn(function() pcall(function() yunoFetchModule(controllers:WaitForChild("CameraController", 4)) end) end)
task.spawn(function() pcall(function() yunoFetchModule(modulesFolder:WaitForChild("InputLibrary", 4)) end) end)
end

local function yunoSuppressPrompts()
local coreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local robloxGui = coreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
if robloxGui then
local errorPrompt = robloxGui:FindFirstChild("ErrorPrompt")
if errorPrompt and errorPrompt:IsA("GuiObject") then
pcall(function() errorPrompt.Visible = false end)
end
end
local promptGui = coreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxPromptGui")
if promptGui and promptGui:IsA("GuiObject") then
promptGui.Visible = false
end
end

local function YunoRunStrap()
task.spawn(function()
pcall(yunoWarmupModules)
end)
end

-- FPS cap unlock (unconstrained)

local function yunoStrReplace(str, find, replace)
local startPos = 1
while true do
local s, e = str:find(find, startPos, true)
if not s then break end
str = str:sub(1, s - 1) .. replace .. str:sub(e + 1)
startPos = s + #replace
end
return str
end

getgenv().YunoSlideTable = getgenv().YunoSlideTable or {}
getgenv().YunoSlideSpeed = getgenv().YunoSlideSpeed or 11

getgenv().yunoSlideLerp = function(fill, targetX, hideBorder, sliderObj, forceSnap)
if not fill or not fill.Parent then return end
targetX = math.floor(tonumber(targetX) or 0)
local stateMap = getgenv().YunoSlideTable
if forceSnap then
fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, targetX, 1, 0)
if hideBorder and sliderObj then
hideBorder.Visible = not (targetX == sliderObj.MaxSize or targetX == 0)
end
stateMap[fill] = nil
return
end
local st = stateMap[fill]
if st then
st.target = targetX
st.hideBorder = hideBorder
st.sliderObj = sliderObj
else
stateMap[fill] = { target = targetX, pos = fill.Size.X.Offset, hideBorder = hideBorder, sliderObj = sliderObj }
end
end

if not getgenv().YunoSlideBound then
getgenv().YunoSlideBound = true
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
local stateMap = getgenv().YunoSlideTable
if not stateMap then return end
dt = math.clamp(tonumber(dt) or 0, 1 / 1000, 1 / 15)
local follow = getgenv().YunoSlideSpeed or 11
local step = 1 - math.exp(-follow * dt)
for fill, st in pairs(stateMap) do
if not fill.Parent then
stateMap[fill] = nil
else
st.pos = st.pos + (st.target - st.pos) * step
if math.abs(st.target - st.pos) < 0.35 then st.pos = st.target end
local x = math.floor(st.pos + 0.5)
fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, x, 1, 0)
if st.hideBorder and st.sliderObj then
st.hideBorder.Visible = not (x == st.sliderObj.MaxSize or x == 0)
end
end
end
end)
end

local function yunoLoad(url, name, mustContain)
local src
local localPaths = {
"yuno ui lib/" .. name .. ".lua",
"yuno ui folder/" .. name .. ".lua",
"yuno ui/" .. name .. ".lua",
"yuno/" .. name .. ".lua",
name .. ".lua"
}
for _, path in ipairs(localPaths) do
if isfile and isfile(path) then
local ok, content = pcall(readfile, path)
if ok and content and #content > 0 and (not mustContain or content:find(mustContain, 1, true)) then
src = content
break
elseif ok and content and mustContain then
warn("[Yuno] local " .. path .. " is outdated, falling back to remote")
end
end
end
if not src then
local ok, res = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, url)
if ok and res and #res > 0 then
src = res
end
end
if not src then warn("[Yuno] Failed to get source for " .. name) return nil end
local fn, err = loadstring(src)
if not fn then warn("[Yuno] loadstring failed for " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(err)) return nil end
local res, ret = pcall(fn)
if not res then warn("[Yuno] Runtime error in " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(ret)) return nil end
return ret
end

}

end

getgenv().YunoCustomCursor = getgenv().YunoCustomCursor or {}

local function ensureInstanceMenuCursor()
local mc = getgenv().YunoCustomCursor
if mc.cursor then pcall(function() mc.cursor:Remove() end) end
if mc.outline then pcall(function() mc.outline:Remove() end) end
mc.cursor = Drawing.new("Triangle")
mc.cursor.Filled = true
mc.cursor.Thickness = 1
mc.cursor.Visible = false
mc.outline = Drawing.new("Triangle")
mc.outline.Filled = false
mc.outline.Thickness = 1
mc.outline.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
mc.outline.Visible = false
mc.initialized = true
mc.wasOpen = false
mc.savedMouseIcon = true
end

local function isInstanceMenuOpen()
if not Library or Library.Unloaded then return false end
if Window and Window.Holder and Window.Holder.Parent then
return Window.Holder.Visible
end
return false
end

local function shouldSuppressGameplayOverlays()
return isInstanceMenuOpen()
end

ensureInstanceMenuCursor()

local MENU_CURSOR_HZ = 240
local MENU_CURSOR_BIND = "YunoCursorRender"
local menuCursorAccum = 0

local function drawInstanceMenuCursor()
local mc = getgenv().YunoCustomCursor
if not mc or not mc.cursor or not mc.outline then return end
local open = isInstanceMenuOpen()
if not open then
if mc.wasOpen then
mc.cursor.Visible = false
mc.outline.Visible = false
UserInputServiceMenu.MouseIconEnabled = mc.savedMouseIcon
mc.wasOpen = false
end
return
end
if not mc.wasOpen then
mc.savedMouseIcon = UserInputServiceMenu.MouseIconEnabled
end
UserInputServiceMenu.MouseIconEnabled = true
if mc.cursor.Visible then
mc.cursor.Visible = false
mc.outline.Visible = false
end
mc.wasOpen = true
end

pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(MENU_CURSOR_BIND) end)
pcall(function()
RunService:BindToRenderStep(MENU_CURSOR_BIND, Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value + 10, function(dt)
menuCursorAccum = menuCursorAccum + (dt or 0)
local step = 1 / MENU_CURSOR_HZ
if menuCursorAccum < step then return end
menuCursorAccum = menuCursorAccum % step
drawInstanceMenuCursor()
end)
end)

getgenv().yunoLocalizeWeapon = function(name)
if not name or type(name) ~= "string" then return name end
local lang = getgenv().YunoCurrentLang or "english"
if lang == "english" then return name end
local pack = YunoWeaponDict[lang]
return (pack and pack[name]) or name
end

getgenv().yunoLocalizeText = function(text)
if not text or type(text) ~= "string" then return text end
local lang = getgenv().YunoCurrentLang or "english"
if lang == "english" then return text:lower() end
local translated = getgenv().yunoLocalizeWeapon(text)
if translated ~= text then return translated:lower() end
return text:lower()
end

getgenv().yunoResolveFont = function()
if getgenv().YunoCurrentLang == "korean" and getgenv().YunoActiveFont then
return getgenv().YunoActiveFont
end
if Library and Library.Font then
return Library.Font
end
return getgenv().YunoActiveFont or Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Roboto)
end

getgenv().YunoL = function(key, ...)
local lang = getgenv().YunoCurrentLang or "english"
local pack = YunoDictionary[lang] or YunoDictionary.english
local template = pack[key] or YunoDictionary.english[key] or key
if select("#", ...) > 0 then
return string.format(template, ...)
end
return template
end

local yunoFetchFont = game:GetService("HttpService")

local YunoFontRepo = {
english = { file = "yuno_font_en.otf", fontFile = "yuno_font_en.font", name = "YunoFontEn", url = "https://github.com/nyrus573l/esp-fonts/raw/refs/heads/main/fortnite.otf" },
korean = { file = "yuno_font_ko.otf", fontFile = "yuno_font_ko.font", name = "YunoFontKo", url = "https://github.com/googlefonts/noto-cjk/raw/main/Sans/OTF/Korean/NotoSansCJKkr-Regular.otf" },
spanish = { file = "yuno_font_es.otf", fontFile = "yuno_font_es.font", name = "YunoFontEs", url = "https://github.com/nyrus573l/esp-fonts/raw/refs/heads/main/fortnite.otf" },
}

local function loadInstanceLanguageFont(lang)
lang = lang or getgenv().YunoCurrentLang or "english"
local src = YunoFontRepo[lang] or YunoFontRepo.english
local ok, face = pcall(function()
if isfile and writefile and getcustomasset then
if not isfile(src.file) then
writefile(src.file, game:HttpGet(src.url))
end
if isfile(src.fontFile) then pcall(function() delfile(src.fontFile) end) end
local fontdata = { name = src.name, faces = {{ name = "Regular", weight = 400, style = "normal", assetId = getcustomasset(src.file) }} }
writefile(src.fontFile, yunoFetchFont:JSONEncode(fontdata))
return Font.new(getcustomasset(src.fontFile))
end
return nil
end)
if ok and face then
getgenv().YunoActiveFont = face
return face
end
getgenv().YunoActiveFont = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Medium)
return getgenv().YunoActiveFont
end

getgenv().yunoReloadTypeface = function(lang)
lang = lang or getgenv().YunoCurrentLang or "english"
if lang == "english" and Library and Library.Font then
getgenv().YunoActiveFont = Library.Font
return Library.Font
end
return loadInstanceLanguageFont(lang)
end
task.defer(function()
pcall(function()
getgenv().yunoReloadTypeface(getgenv().YunoCurrentLang)
end)
end)

getgenv().YunoTextBuffer = getgenv().YunoTextBuffer or {}

getgenv().yunoRegisterText = function(obj)
table.insert(getgenv().YunoTextBuffer, obj)
pcall(function()
local face = getgenv().YunoActiveFont
if face and obj.FontFace ~= nil then obj.FontFace = face end
end)
return obj
end

getgenv().yunoSetDrawingFace = function()
local face = getgenv().YunoActiveFont
if not face then return end
for _, obj in ipairs(getgenv().YunoTextBuffer) do
pcall(function()
if obj.FontFace ~= nil then obj.FontFace = face end
end)
end
end

getgenv().YunoApplyUiFont = function(obj, textSize)
if not obj then return end
if textSize then obj.TextSize = textSize end
local font = getgenv().yunoResolveFont()
pcall(function()
if typeof(font) == "Font" then
obj.FontFace = font
else
obj.Font = font
end
end)
end

getgenv().yunoApplyTypography = function()
local face = getgenv().yunoResolveFont()
if not face then return end
getgenv().YunoActiveFont = face
if _G.ESPObjects then
for _, box in pairs(_G.ESPObjects) do
if box and box.text then
for _, lbl in pairs(box.text) do
if typeof(lbl) == "Instance" and lbl:IsA("TextLabel") then
lbl.FontFace = face
end
end
end
end
end
if getgenv().yunoSetDrawingFace then pcall(getgenv().yunoSetDrawingFace) end
end

getgenv().YunoHudFontRegistry = {
{ n = "gotham", draw = 2, face = function() return Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Medium) end },
{ n = "code", draw = 3, face = function() return Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Code) end },
{ n = "roboto", draw = 1, face = function() return Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Roboto) end },
{ n = "builder", draw = 0, face = function() return Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.BuilderSans) end },
{ n = "arial", draw = 1, face = function() return Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Arial) end },
{ n = "legacy", draw = 0, face = function() return Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Legacy) end },
{ n = "source", draw = 2, face = function() return Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.SourceSans) end },
{ n = "custom", draw = 2, face = function() return getgenv().YunoActiveFont or Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Gotham) end },
}
getgenv().YunoHudFontCatalog = {}
for i, slot in ipairs(getgenv().YunoHudFontRegistry) do
getgenv().YunoHudFontCatalog[i] = slot.n
end
getgenv().YunoHudFontPointer = 1
getgenv().YunoHudFontData = 2

getgenv().yunoFindHudFont = function(name)
for i, slot in ipairs(getgenv().YunoHudFontRegistry) do
if slot.n == name then return i end
end
return 1
end

getgenv().yunoSyncHudTypography = function(idx)
idx = math.clamp(tonumber(idx) or getgenv().YunoHudFontPointer or 1, 1, #getgenv().YunoHudFontRegistry)
local slot = getgenv().YunoHudFontRegistry[idx]
getgenv().YunoHudFontPointer = idx
getgenv().YunoHudFontData = slot.draw or 2
local face
pcall(function() face = slot.face() end)
if face then getgenv().YunoEspTypeface = face end
if _G.ESPObjects then
for _, box in pairs(_G.ESPObjects) do
if box and box.text then
for _, lbl in pairs(box.text) do
if typeof(lbl) == "Instance" and lbl:IsA("TextLabel") and face then
lbl.FontFace = face
end
end
end
end
end
for _, obj in ipairs(getgenv().YunoTextBuffer or {}) do
pcall(function()
if obj.Font ~= nil then obj.Font = getgenv().YunoHudFontData end
if face and obj.FontFace ~= nil then obj.FontFace = face end
end)
end
end

local Tabs = {
Combat = Window:AddTab("rage"),
Character = Window:AddTab("player"),
Visuals = Window:AddTab("visuals"),
World = Window:AddTab("world"),
Misc = Window:AddTab("misc"),
Skins = Window:AddTab("skins"),
['UI Settings'] = Window:AddTab('ui'),
}

local function alignInstanceMenuTabs()
if not Window or not Window.Holder or Window.ModularWindows then return end
local inner = Window.Holder:FindFirstChildOfClass("Frame")
if not inner then return end

local tabArea, tabLayout, titleLabel
for _, child in ipairs(inner:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("TextLabel") and child.TextXAlignment == Enum.TextXAlignment.Left then
titleLabel = child
elseif child:IsA("Frame") then
local layout = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
if layout and layout.FillDirection == Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal then
tabArea = child
tabLayout = layout
end
end
end

if not tabArea then return end

local titleX = titleLabel and titleLabel.Position.X.Offset or 12
local titleWidth = 0
if titleLabel then
pcall(function()
titleWidth = titleLabel.TextBounds.X
end)
if titleWidth <= 0 then
titleWidth = 52
end
end

tabArea.Position = UDim2.new(0, titleX + titleWidth + 10, 0, 4)
tabArea.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(titleX + titleWidth + 18), 0, 22)
tabArea.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)

if tabLayout then
tabLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
tabLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, Window.TabPadding or 6)
end
end

task.defer(alignInstanceMenuTabs)

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Players           = game:GetService("Players")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Camera            = workspace.CurrentCamera
local UserInputService  = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local EnumLibrary       = yunoFetchModule(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.EnumLibrary)
local LocalPlayer       = Players.LocalPlayer

_G.AspectRatioSettings = _G.AspectRatioSettings or {
Enabled = false,
X = 13,
Y = 10,
}

local function getAspectStretch()
local s = _G.AspectRatioSettings
if s and s.Enabled then
return s.X / s.Y
end
return 1
end

local function applyAspectToViewport(screenVec, cam)
local stretch = getAspectStretch()
if stretch == 1 then
return screenVec
end
cam = cam or workspace.CurrentCamera
if not cam then
return screenVec
end
local centerY = cam.ViewportSize.Y * 0.5
return Vector3.new(screenVec.X, centerY + (screenVec.Y - centerY) * stretch, screenVec.Z)
end

local function worldToScreen(worldPos, cam)
cam = cam or workspace.CurrentCamera
if not cam or not worldPos then
return nil, false
end
local v, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(worldPos)
if not onScreen or v.Z <= 0 then
return v, false
end
return applyAspectToViewport(v, cam), true
end

local function screenAnchor(xNorm, yNorm, cam)
cam = cam or workspace.CurrentCamera
if not cam then
return Vector2.zero
end
local vs = cam.ViewportSize
local stretch = getAspectStretch()
local centerY = vs.Y * 0.5
local y = centerY + (vs.Y * yNorm - centerY) * stretch
return Vector2.new(vs.X * xNorm, y)
end

local function screenCenter(cam)
return screenAnchor(0.5, 0.5, cam)
end

local function getUnstretchedCameraCFrame(cam)
cam = cam or workspace.CurrentCamera
if not cam then
return nil
end
local cf = cam.CFrame
if getAspectStretch() == 1 then
return cf
end
local pos = cf.Position
local look = cf.LookVector
local right = cf.RightVector
local up = right:Cross(look).Unit
return CFrame.fromMatrix(pos, right, up, -look)
end

getgenv().yunoCalcViewportRatio = getAspectStretch
getgenv().yunoProjectWorld = worldToScreen
getgenv().YunoViewportOrigin = screenAnchor
getgenv().YunoViewportCenter = screenCenter
getgenv().yunoGetRawCameraCFrame = getUnstretchedCameraCFrame

_G.Features = _G.Features or {}
_G.Features.CrossbowSoundId = "rbxassetid://165946246"

getgenv().YunoShotTimestamp = tick()
end
getgenv().yunoLogShotFired = markCombatShot

getgenv().yunoProjectEsp = function(worldPos, cam)
return worldToScreen(worldPos, cam)
end

local antikatana = false
local katanausers = {}

local function detectkatana()
local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
if not lp then return end
local ps = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts") or lp:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 2)
if not ps then return end
task.spawn(function()
local katana, attempts = nil, 0
while attempts < 10 do
pcall(function()
local m = lp.PlayerScripts.Modules.Items:FindFirstChild("Katana", true)
if m then katana = require(m) end
end)
if not katana then
for _, m in pairs(lp.PlayerScripts:GetDescendants()) do
if m.Name == "Katana" and m:IsA("ModuleScript") then
local ok, res = pcall(require, m)
if ok then katana = res; break end
end
end
end
if katana and type(katana) == "table" and katana.StartAiming then break end
attempts += 1
task.wait(1)
end
if katana and type(katana) == "table" and katana.StartAiming then
local old = katana.StartAiming
katana.StartAiming = function(self, force)
local fighter = self.ClientFighter
local player  = fighter and fighter.Player
if player then
katanausers[player] = true
local dur = self.Info.DeflectDuration or 0.6
task.delay(dur, function() katanausers[player] = nil end)
end
return old(self, force)
end
end
end)
end
detectkatana()

local function katanadeflect(player)
return katanausers[player] == true
end

local function weaponstricted(weaponName)
if not weaponName then return false end
for _, w in ipairs(restricteditems) do
if weaponName == w then return true end
end
return false
end

local cachedWeapon = nil
local function curweap2()
if cachedWeapon then return cachedWeapon end
local vm = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
if not vm then return nil end
local fp = vm:FindFirstChild("FirstPerson")
if not fp then return nil end
for _, child in ipairs(fp:GetChildren()) do
local name = child.Name
local dash = name:find("-")
if dash then
cachedWeapon = name:sub(dash + 1):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
return cachedWeapon
end
end
return nil
end
local function resetWeaponCache() cachedWeapon = nil end
task.spawn(function()
local vm = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
if vm then
vm.DescendantAdded:Connect(resetWeaponCache)
vm.DescendantRemoving:Connect(resetWeaponCache)
end
end)

local fovScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
fovScreenGui.Name            = "FOVScreenGui"
fovScreenGui.DisplayOrder    = YunoOverlayZIndex
fovScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn    = false
fovScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset  = true
fovScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior  = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
do
local parented = false
pcall(function()
if gethui then
local h = gethui()
if h then fovScreenGui.Parent = h; parented = true end
end
end)
if not parented then
pcall(function() fovScreenGui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui"); parented = true end)
end
if not parented then
pcall(function() fovScreenGui.Parent = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5); parented = true end)
end
if not parented then
warn("[Yuno] FOV gui could not be parented anywhere")
end
end

local function buildfov(name, cfg)
local container = Instance.new("Frame")
container.Name                   = name
container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
container.BorderSizePixel        = 0
container.Visible                = false
container.Parent                 = fovScreenGui

local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
fill.Size                   = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
fill.BackgroundColor3       = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
fill.BackgroundTransparency = cfg.FilledTransparency
fill.BorderSizePixel        = 0
fill.Visible                = false
fill.ZIndex                 = 1
fill.Parent                 = container

local fillCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
fillCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
fillCorner.Parent       = fill

local fillgrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
fillgrad.Color    = ColorSequence.new({
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, cfg.FilledColor1),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, cfg.FilledColor2),
})
fillgrad.Rotation = cfg.FilledRotation
fillgrad.Parent   = fill

local outline = Instance.new("Frame")
outline.Size                   = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
outline.BackgroundTransparency = 1
outline.BorderSizePixel        = 0
outline.ZIndex                 = 2
outline.Parent                 = container

local outlineCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
outlineCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
outlineCorner.Parent       = outline

local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
stroke.Color           = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
stroke.Thickness       = cfg.OutlineThickness
stroke.Transparency    = cfg.OutlineTransparency
stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
stroke.Parent          = outline

local strokegrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
strokegrad.Color    = ColorSequence.new({
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, cfg.OutlineColor1),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, cfg.OutlineColor2),
})
strokegrad.Rotation = cfg.OutlineRotation
strokegrad.Parent   = stroke

return {
container      = container,
fill           = fill,
fillgrad   = fillgrad,
stroke         = stroke,
strokegrad = strokegrad,
}
end
