local function findChild(root, ...)
local node = root
for _, name in ipairs({ ... }) do
if not node then
return nil
end
node = node:FindFirstChild(name)
end
return node
end

local function trim(value)
if type(value) ~= "string" then
return ""
end
return value:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
end

local function normalizeSoundSource(value)
local source = trim(value)
if source == "" then
return nil
end
if source:match("^%d+$") then
return "rbxassetid://" .. source
end
return source
end

local function hashSoundUrl(source)
local hash = 0
for i = 1, #source do
hash = (hash * 31 + string.byte(source, i)) % 2147483647
end
return tostring(hash)
end

local function resolveCustomAssetFn()
if type(getcustomasset) == "function" then
return getcustomasset
end
if type(getsynasset) == "function" then
return getsynasset
end
return nil
end

local function resolveSoundAsset(name)
if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then
return nil
end
local cached = Feedback.SoundAssetCache[name]
if cached ~= nil then
return cached ~= false and cached or nil
end
local source = nil
for _, entry in ipairs(Feedback.BuiltInSounds) do
if entry.name == name then
source = entry.soundId
break
end
end
if not source and Feedback.CustomSounds[name] then
source = Feedback.CustomSounds[name]
end
if not source then
source = normalizeSoundSource(name)
if source and not (source:match("^rbxassetid") or source:match("^https?://") or source:match("%.mp3$") or source:match("%.ogg$") or source:match("%.wav$")) then
source = nil
end
end
if not source then
Feedback.SoundAssetCache[name] = false
return nil
end
if source:match("^rbxasset") then
Feedback.SoundAssetCache[name] = source
return source
end

local customAsset = resolveCustomAssetFn()
if source:match("^https?://") then
if type(writefile) ~= "function" or not customAsset then
Feedback.SoundAssetCache[name] = false
return nil
end
local extension = source:match("(%.[%w]+)[%?#]") or source:match("(%.[%w]+)$") or ".mp3"
extension = extension:lower()
if extension ~= ".mp3" and extension ~= ".ogg" and extension ~= ".wav" then
extension = ".mp3"
end
local cachePath = string.format("%s/%s%s", Feedback.CustomSoundsCacheFolder, hashSoundUrl(source), extension)
local ok = pcall(function()
if type(makefolder) == "function" and type(isfolder) == "function" then
if not isfolder("yuno") then
makefolder("yuno")
end
if not isfolder(Feedback.CustomSoundsCacheFolder) then
makefolder(Feedback.CustomSoundsCacheFolder)
end
end
if type(isfile) ~= "function" or not isfile(cachePath) then
local requestFunction = type(request) == "function" and request
or (type(http_request) == "function" and http_request or nil)
local body = nil
if requestFunction then
local response = requestFunction({ Url = source, Method = "GET" })
local status = tonumber(response and (response.StatusCode or response.Status)) or 0
if status < 200 or status >= 300 then
error("download failed")
end
body = response.Body
else
body = game:HttpGet(source)
end
if type(body) ~= "string" or body == "" then
error("empty download")
end
writefile(cachePath, body)
end
end)
if ok then
local assetOk, asset = pcall(customAsset, cachePath)
if assetOk and type(asset) == "string" and asset ~= "" then
Feedback.SoundAssetCache[name] = asset
return asset
end
end
Feedback.SoundAssetCache[name] = false
return nil
end

if customAsset and type(isfile) == "function" and isfile(source) then
local ok, asset = pcall(customAsset, source)
if ok and type(asset) == "string" and asset ~= "" then
Feedback.SoundAssetCache[name] = asset
return asset
end
end
Feedback.SoundAssetCache[name] = false
return nil
end

function Feedback.EnsureCustomSoundsLoaded()
if Feedback.CustomSoundsLoaded then
return
end
Feedback.CustomSoundsLoaded = true
Feedback.CustomSounds = {}
if type(isfile) ~= "function" or not isfile(Feedback.CustomSoundsPath) then
return
end
local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
return fbHttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(Feedback.CustomSoundsPath))
end)
local records = ok and type(decoded) == "table" and (decoded.sounds or decoded) or nil
if type(records) ~= "table" then
return
end
local seen = {}
for _, record in ipairs(records) do
if type(record) == "table" then
local name = trim(record.name)
local source = normalizeSoundSource(record.soundId or record.source)
if name ~= "" and source and not seen[name] then
seen[name] = true
Feedback.CustomSounds[name] = source
end
end
end
end

function Feedback.GetSoundNames()
Feedback.EnsureCustomSoundsLoaded()
local names = {}
local seen = {}
for _, entry in ipairs(Feedback.BuiltInSounds) do
names[#names + 1] = entry.name
seen[entry.name] = true
end
for name in pairs(Feedback.CustomSounds) do
if not seen[name] then
names[#names + 1] = name
end
end
table.sort(names, function(a, b) return a:lower() < b:lower() end)
return names
end

function Feedback.PlaySound(name, volume, pitch)
local asset = resolveSoundAsset(name)
if not asset then
return
end
local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
sound.Name = "YunoCombatFeedback"
sound.SoundId = asset
sound.Volume = math.max(0, tonumber(volume) or 1)
sound.PlaybackSpeed = math.max(0.01, tonumber(pitch) or 1)
sound.Parent = fbSoundService
fbDebris:AddItem(sound, 20)
sound:Play()
end

function Feedback.CreateChams(character, color, transparency, duration, materialName)
if typeof(character) ~= "Instance" or not character:IsA("Model") then
return
end
local model = Instance.new("Model")
model.Name = "YunoCombatFeedbackChams"
local parts = {}
local material = Feedback.MaterialMap[materialName] or Enum.Material.ForceField
local baseTransparency = math.clamp(tonumber(transparency) or 0, 0, 1)
for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" and part.Transparency < 1 then
local ok, ghost = pcall(function()
return part:Clone()
end)
if not ok or not ghost or not ghost:IsA("BasePart") then
ghost = Instance.new("Part")
ghost.Size = part.Size
ghost.Shape = part:IsA("Part") and part.Shape or Enum.PartType.Block
end
for _, descendant in ipairs(ghost:GetDescendants()) do
if not descendant:IsA("DataModelMesh") then
descendant:Destroy()
end
end
ghost.Name = part.Name
ghost.Anchored = true
ghost.CanCollide = false
ghost.CanQuery = false
ghost.CanTouch = false
ghost.CastShadow = false
ghost.Color = typeof(color) == "Color3" and color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
ghost.Material = material
ghost.Transparency = baseTransparency
ghost.CFrame = part.CFrame
pcall(function()
ghost.MaterialVariant = ""
end)
ghost.Parent = model
table.insert(parts, { Source = part, Ghost = ghost })
end
end
if #parts == 0 then
model:Destroy()
return
end
model.Parent = workspace
table.insert(Feedback.Chams, {
Model = model,
Parts = parts,
StartedAt = tick(),
Duration = math.max(0.05, tonumber(duration) or 0.8),
Transparency = baseTransparency,
})
end

function Feedback.SetGameHitsoundDisabled(disabled)
if disabled then
if Feedback.NativeHitsoundRecord then
return
end
local viewModelModule = findChild(fbReplicatedStorage, "Modules", "ClientReplicatedClasses", "ClientFighter", "ClientItem", "ClientViewModel")
if not viewModelModule then
return
end
local ok, viewModel = pcall(require, viewModelModule)
if not ok or type(viewModel) ~= "table" then
return
end
local method = rawget(viewModel, "PlayHitmarkerSound")
if method == nil then
return
end
rawset(viewModel, "PlayHitmarkerSound", function() end)
Feedback.NativeHitsoundRecord = { class = viewModel, method = method }
else
local record = Feedback.NativeHitsoundRecord
if record then
Feedback.NativeHitsoundRecord = nil
pcall(function()
rawset(record.class, "PlayHitmarkerSound", record.method)
end)
end
end
end

local tracerSpringClass = nil
local function resolveTracerSpringClass()
if tracerSpringClass ~= nil then
return tracerSpringClass or nil
end
local modulesFolder = fbReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
local springModule = modulesFolder and modulesFolder:FindFirstChild("Spring") or nil
local ok, springClass = false, nil
if springModule then
ok, springClass = pcall(require, springModule)
end
tracerSpringClass = ok and type(springClass) == "table" and springClass or false
return tracerSpringClass or nil
end

local function createTracer(startPosition, finishPosition)
if typeof(startPosition) ~= "Vector3" or typeof(finishPosition) ~= "Vector3" then
return
end
local springClass = resolveTracerSpringClass()
if not springClass or type(springClass.new) ~= "function" then
return
end
while #Feedback.Tracers >= Feedback.MaxTracers do
local oldest = table.remove(Feedback.Tracers, 1)
if oldest then
pcall(function()
oldest.Beam:Destroy()
oldest.Near:Destroy()
oldest.Far:Destroy()
end)
end
end
local fx = config.hitfx
local near = Instance.new("Attachment")
near.Position = startPosition
near.Parent = workspace.Terrain
local far = Instance.new("Attachment")
far.Position = startPosition
far.Parent = workspace.Terrain
local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
beam.Name = "YunoBulletTracer"
beam.Attachment0 = near
beam.Attachment1 = far
beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(typeof(fx.tracerColor) == "Color3" and fx.tracerColor or Color3.fromRGB(120, 220, 255))
beam.Width0 = tonumber(fx.tracerWidth) or 0.06
beam.Width1 = beam.Width0
beam.FaceCamera = true
beam.LightEmission = tonumber(fx.tracerLightEmission) or 1
beam.LightInfluence = 0
local glow = math.clamp(tonumber(fx.tracerGlow) or 1, 1, 25)
beam.Brightness = 10000 ^ ((glow - 1) / 24)
beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
local texture = Feedback.TracerStyles[fx.tracerStyle] or ""
if texture ~= "" then
beam.Texture = texture
beam.TextureLength = tonumber(fx.tracerTexLength) or 4
beam.TextureSpeed = tonumber(fx.tracerTexSpeed) or 1
end
beam.Parent = workspace.Terrain

local spring = springClass.new(0)
spring.Speed = tonumber(fx.tracerSpringSpeed) or 18
spring.Damper = tonumber(fx.tracerSpringDamper) or 0.7
spring.Target = 1
local expand = fx.tracerExpand == true
if not expand then
spring.Position = 1
far.Position = finishPosition
end
table.insert(Feedback.Tracers, {
Beam = beam,
Near = near,
Far = far,
Spring = spring,
Start = startPosition,
Finish = finishPosition,
CreatedAt = tick(),
Lifetime = math.max(0.1, tonumber(fx.tracerLifetime) or 0.6),
FadeTime = math.max(0, tonumber(fx.tracerFadeTime) or 0.35),
Expand = expand,
})
end

local cachedFighterControllerFB = nil
local function resolveFighterControllerFB()
local cc = cachedFighterControllerFB
if type(cc) == "table" and rawget(cc, "LocalFighter") ~= nil then
return cc
end
for _, m in ipairs(getgc(true)) do
if type(m) == "table" and rawget(m, "LocalFighter") ~= nil and rawget(m, "Objects") ~= nil then
cachedFighterControllerFB = m
return m
end
end
return nil
end
local function resolveLocalFighterFB()
local controller = resolveFighterControllerFB()
return controller and rawget(controller, "LocalFighter") or nil
end
local function readValue(obj, key)
if type(obj) == "table" then
local v = rawget(obj, key)
if v ~= nil then
return v
end
local ok, result = pcall(function() return obj[key] end)
if ok then
return result
end
elseif typeof(obj) == "Instance" then
local ok, result = pcall(function() return obj:GetAttribute(key) end)
if ok then
return result
end
end
return nil
end
local function resolveFighterObjectId(fighter)
local data = type(fighter) == "table" and rawget(fighter, "Data") or nil
local objectId = type(data) == "table" and rawget(data, "ObjectID") or nil
return objectId or readValue(fighter, "ObjectID")
end
local function resolveItemByObjectId(objectId)
local function findIn(fighter)
if type(fighter) ~= "table" then
return nil
end
local items = rawget(fighter, "Items")
if type(items) ~= "table" then
return nil
end
for _, item in pairs(items) do
local data = type(item) == "table" and rawget(item, "Data") or nil
local itemObjectId = type(data) == "table" and rawget(data, "ObjectID") or nil
if (itemObjectId or readValue(item, "ObjectID")) == objectId then
return item
end
end
return nil
end
local item = findIn(resolveLocalFighterFB())
if item then
return item
end
local fighterController = resolveFighterControllerFB()
local objects = fighterController and rawget(fighterController, "Objects") or nil
if type(objects) == "table" then
for _, fighter in pairs(objects) do
item = findIn(fighter)
if item then
return item
end
end
end
return nil
end

local function resolveEnumLibraryFB()
local mod = findChild(fbReplicatedStorage, "Modules", "EnumLibrary")
if not mod then
return nil
end
local ok, lib = pcall(require, mod)
if ok and type(lib) == "table" then
return lib
end
return nil
end
local function tryResolveEnumTokens()
if Feedback.EnumReady then
return true
end
local enumLibrary = resolveEnumLibraryFB()
if not enumLibrary or enumLibrary._enum_builder_complete ~= true or type(enumLibrary.ToEnum) ~= "function" then
return false
end
for _, enumName in ipairs({ "ShootEffect", "DamageNumberEffect", "EliminationEffect", "FinisherEffect" }) do
local ok, token = pcall(enumLibrary.ToEnum, enumLibrary, enumName)
if ok and token ~= nil then
Feedback.EnumTokens[enumName] = token
end
end
Feedback.EnumReady = next(Feedback.EnumTokens) ~= nil
return Feedback.EnumReady
end

local function handleHit(damage, partName, character)
local fx = config.hitfx
if not fx.enabled then
return
end
if fx.soundEnabled then
local isHead = partName == "Head"
local soundName = isHead and fx.headSound or nil
if type(soundName) ~= "string" or soundName == "" then
soundName = fx.bodySound
end
Feedback.PlaySound(
soundName,
isHead and fx.headVolume or fx.bodyVolume,
isHead and fx.headPitch or fx.bodyPitch
)
end
if fx.chamsEnabled then
Feedback.CreateChams(
character,
fx.chamColor,
tonumber(fx.chamTransparency) or 0,
tonumber(fx.chamDuration) or 0.8,
fx.chamMaterial
)
end
end

local function handleKill(character)
local fx = config.hitfx
if not fx.killEnabled then
return
end
if fx.killSoundEnabled then
Feedback.PlaySound(fx.killSound, fx.killVolume, fx.killPitch)
end
if fx.killChamsEnabled then
Feedback.CreateChams(
character,
fx.killChamColor,
0,
tonumber(fx.killChamDuration) or 0.8,
fx.killChamMaterial
)
end
end

local function resolveEffectPacket(packet)
for index = 3, packet.n do
local value = packet[index]
local matched = nil
if type(value) == "string" then
for name, token in pairs(Feedback.EnumTokens) do
if value == token then
matched = name
break
end
end
end
if matched then
local objectId = (index == 4) and packet[2] or packet[index - 1]
if type(objectId) == "string" then
return matched, objectId, index
end
end
end
return nil
end

local function handleShootEffect(objectId, raycastResults)
local fx = config.hitfx
if not fx.tracerEnabled or type(raycastResults) ~= "table" then
return
end
local item = resolveItemByObjectId(objectId)
local viewModel = type(item) == "table" and rawget(item, "ViewModel") or nil
if type(viewModel) ~= "table" or type(viewModel.GetMuzzlePosition) ~= "function" then
return
end
local ok, muzzlePosition = pcall(viewModel.GetMuzzlePosition, viewModel)
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
if not ok or typeof(muzzlePosition) ~= "Vector3" or not camera then
return
end
local startPosition = muzzlePosition + camera.CFrame.LookVector
for _, encodedResult in pairs(raycastResults) do
local finishPosition = type(encodedResult) == "table" and encodedResult[utf8.char(0)] or nil
if typeof(finishPosition) == "Vector3" then
createTracer(startPosition, finishPosition)
end
end
end

local function handleReplicate(...)
if not (config.hitfx.enabled or config.hitfx.killEnabled or config.hitfx.tracerEnabled) then
return
end
if not Feedback.EnumReady and not tryResolveEnumTokens() then
return
end
local packet = table.pack(...)
local effectName, objectId, index = resolveEffectPacket(packet)
if not effectName then
return
end
if effectName == "ShootEffect" then
handleShootEffect(objectId, packet[index + 1])
elseif effectName == "DamageNumberEffect" and config.hitfx.enabled then
local localFighter = resolveLocalFighterFB()
if not localFighter or resolveFighterObjectId(localFighter) ~= objectId then
return
end
local hitPart, damage, isHead = packet[index + 1], packet[index + 2], packet[index + 3]
if typeof(hitPart) ~= "Instance" then
return
end
local character = hitPart.Parent
if typeof(character) ~= "Instance" or not character:IsA("Model") then
return
end
handleHit(damage, isHead == true and "Head" or "Body", character)
elseif effectName == "EliminationEffect" and config.hitfx.killEnabled then
local localFighter = resolveLocalFighterFB()
if not localFighter or resolveFighterObjectId(localFighter) ~= objectId then
return
end
local eliminated = packet[index + 1]
if eliminated == LPFB or (LPFB.Character and eliminated == LPFB.Character) then
return
end
local character = nil
if typeof(eliminated) == "Instance" then
if eliminated:IsA("Player") then
character = eliminated.Character
elseif eliminated:IsA("Model") then
character = eliminated
end
end
handleKill(character)
elseif effectName == "FinisherEffect" and config.hitfx.killEnabled then
if packet[index + 3] ~= LPFB then
return
end
local character = nil
local controllers = { resolveFighterControllerFB() }
for _, controller in ipairs(controllers) do
local objects = controller and rawget(controller, "Objects") or nil
if type(objects) == "table" then
for _, subject in ipairs(objects) do
if resolveFighterObjectId(subject) == objectId then
local entity = type(subject) == "table" and rawget(subject, "Entity") or nil
local model = type(entity) == "table" and rawget(entity, "Model") or nil
if typeof(model) == "Instance" and model:IsA("Model") then
character = model
break
end
local fplayer = type(subject) == "table" and rawget(subject, "Player") or nil
if typeof(fplayer) == "Instance" and fplayer:IsA("Player") then
character = fplayer.Character
break
end
end
end
end
if character then break end
end
handleKill(character)
end
end

function Feedback.Unbind()
for key, conn in pairs(Feedback.Connections) do
pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
Feedback.Connections[key] = nil
end
Feedback.BindingConnected = false
end

function Feedback.TryBind()
if Feedback.BindingConnected then
return
end
local remotes = fbReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
local replication = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Replication") or nil
local fighter = replication and replication:FindFirstChild("Fighter") or nil
local enemy = replication and replication:FindFirstChild("Enemy") or nil
local replicate = fighter and fighter:FindFirstChild("Replicate") or nil
local unreliable = fighter and fighter:FindFirstChild("ReplicateUnreliable") or nil
local enemyReplicate = enemy and enemy:FindFirstChild("Replicate") or nil
local enemyUnreliable = enemy and enemy:FindFirstChild("ReplicateUnreliable") or nil
if not replicate and not unreliable then
return
end
if replicate then
Feedback.Connections.Replicate = replicate.OnClientEvent:Connect(handleReplicate)
end
if unreliable then
Feedback.Connections.ReplicateUnreliable = unreliable.OnClientEvent:Connect(handleReplicate)
end
if enemyReplicate then
Feedback.Connections.EnemyReplicate = enemyReplicate.OnClientEvent:Connect(handleReplicate)
end
if enemyUnreliable then
Feedback.Connections.EnemyUnreliable = enemyUnreliable.OnClientEvent:Connect(handleReplicate)
end
Feedback.BindingConnected = true
end

do
local token = { dead = false }
yunoRegisterLive(function() token.dead = true end)
task.spawn(function()
while not token.dead do
if config.hitfx.enabled or config.hitfx.killEnabled or config.hitfx.tracerEnabled then
pcall(function() Feedback.TryBind() end)
end
task.wait(2)
end
end)
end

yunoRegisterLive(fbRunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if #Feedback.Tracers == 0 and #Feedback.Chams == 0 then
return
end
local now = tick()
for index = #Feedback.Tracers, 1, -1 do
local tracer = Feedback.Tracers[index]
local age = now - tracer.CreatedAt
local expired = age >= tracer.Lifetime + tracer.FadeTime
or not tracer.Beam or tracer.Beam.Parent == nil
if expired then
pcall(function()
tracer.Beam:Destroy()
tracer.Near:Destroy()
tracer.Far:Destroy()
end)
table.remove(Feedback.Tracers, index)
else
local fxColor = config.hitfx.tracerColor
tracer.Beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(typeof(fxColor) == "Color3" and fxColor or Color3.fromRGB(120, 220, 255))
if tracer.Expand then
tracer.Far.Position = tracer.Start:Lerp(tracer.Finish, math.clamp(tracer.Spring.Position, 0, 1))
end
local alpha = tracer.FadeTime > 0
and math.clamp((age - tracer.Lifetime) / tracer.FadeTime, 0, 1) or 0
tracer.Beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(alpha)
end
end
for index = #Feedback.Chams, 1, -1 do
local cham = Feedback.Chams[index]
local progress = math.clamp((now - cham.StartedAt) / cham.Duration, 0, 1)
if progress >= 1 or not cham.Model or cham.Model.Parent == nil then
if cham.Model then
pcall(function()
cham.Model:Destroy()
end)
end
table.remove(Feedback.Chams, index)
else
for partIndex = #cham.Parts, 1, -1 do
local pair = cham.Parts[partIndex]
if pair.Source and pair.Source.Parent and pair.Ghost and pair.Ghost.Parent then
pair.Ghost.CFrame = pair.Source.CFrame
pair.Ghost.Transparency = cham.Transparency + (1 - cham.Transparency) * progress
else
if pair.Ghost then
pcall(function() pair.Ghost:Destroy() end)
end
table.remove(cham.Parts, partIndex)
end
end
end
end
end))

getgenv().YunoSetNoGameHitsound = function(disabled)
pcall(function() Feedback.SetGameHitsoundDisabled(disabled) end)
end
end

getgenv().initYunoCombatFeedback()

getgenv().initYunoStaffDetector = function()
pcall(function()
local prev = getgenv().YunoStaffDetector
if type(prev) == "table" and type(prev.Stop) == "function" then
prev:Stop()
end
end)
local sdPlayers = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
local sdHttpService = cloneref(game:GetService("HttpService"))
local sdTeleportService = cloneref(game:GetService("TeleportService"))
local SDLP = sdPlayers.LocalPlayer
local StaffDetector = {
Notifications = {},
Connections = {},
RoleCache = {},
FriendCache = {},
Detections = {},
ActionCache = {},
ClassificationInFlight = {},
RetryGeneration = {},
StaffUserIds = {},
NotifyThrottle = {},
GroupId = 3461453,
MinimumRank = 100,
LobbyPlaceId = 17625359962,
RoleCacheLifetime = 300,
FriendCacheLifetime = 300,
StaffSetLifetime = 900,
FailureRetryDelay = 15,
MaxStaffRoles = 32,
MaxGroupPagesPerRole = 10,
MaxFriendPages = 5,
StaffSetExpiresAt = 0,
StaffSetReady = false,
StaffSetComplete = false,
StaffSetLoading = false,
EscapeCooldownUntil = 0,
Enabled = false,
Generation = 0,
}
getgenv().YunoStaffDetector = StaffDetector

function StaffDetector.Notify(title, description)
local ok = pcall(function()
Library:Notify(title .. " - " .. description, 8)
end)
if not ok then
warn("[Yuno] " .. title .. ": " .. description)
end
end

function StaffDetector.FetchJson(url)
local requestOk, response = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url) end)
if not requestOk or type(response) ~= "string" then
return nil
end
local decodeOk, decoded = pcall(sdHttpService.JSONDecode, sdHttpService, response)
return decodeOk and type(decoded) == "table" and decoded or nil
end

function StaffDetector.IsStaff(player)
if not player or player == SDLP then return false end
local cached = StaffDetector.RoleCache[player.UserId]
if cached and cached.ExpiresAt > os.clock() then
return cached.Value
end
local ok, rank = pcall(function()
return player:GetRankInGroup(StaffDetector.GroupId)
end)
if not ok or type(rank) ~= "number" then
return nil
end
local isStaff = rank >= StaffDetector.MinimumRank
StaffDetector.RoleCache[player.UserId] = {
Value = isStaff,
ExpiresAt = os.clock() + StaffDetector.RoleCacheLifetime,
}
if isStaff then
StaffDetector.StaffUserIds[player.UserId] = true
end
return isStaff
end

function StaffDetector.EnsureStaffUserIds()
local now = os.clock()
if StaffDetector.StaffSetReady and StaffDetector.StaffSetExpiresAt > now then
return true
end
if StaffDetector.StaffSetLoading then
local waitUntil = now + 5
repeat
task.wait(0.05)
until not StaffDetector.StaffSetLoading or os.clock() >= waitUntil
return StaffDetector.StaffSetReady
end
StaffDetector.StaffSetLoading = true
local roles = StaffDetector.FetchJson(string.format(
"https://groups.roblox.com/v1/groups/%d/roles",
StaffDetector.GroupId
))
local nextStaffUserIds = {}
local complete = roles ~= nil and type(roles.roles) == "table"
local staffRoleCount = 0
if complete then
for _, role in ipairs(roles.roles) do
if type(role) == "table" and type(role.rank) == "number"
and role.rank >= StaffDetector.MinimumRank and tonumber(role.id) then
staffRoleCount = staffRoleCount + 1
if staffRoleCount > StaffDetector.MaxStaffRoles then
complete = false
break
end
local cursor = nil
local pageCount = 0
repeat
pageCount = pageCount + 1
local url = string.format(
"https://groups.roblox.com/v1/groups/%d/roles/%d/users?limit=100&sortOrder=Asc",
StaffDetector.GroupId,
tonumber(role.id)
)
if cursor and cursor ~= "" then
url = url .. "&cursor=" .. sdHttpService:UrlEncode(cursor)
end
local page = StaffDetector.FetchJson(url)
if not page or type(page.data) ~= "table" then
complete = false
break
end
for _, member in ipairs(page.data) do
local userId = type(member) == "table" and tonumber(member.userId or member.id) or nil
if userId then
nextStaffUserIds[userId] = true
end
end
cursor = page.nextPageCursor
until not cursor or cursor == "" or pageCount >= StaffDetector.MaxGroupPagesPerRole
if cursor and cursor ~= "" then
complete = false
end
end
end
end
if complete then
StaffDetector.StaffUserIds = nextStaffUserIds
StaffDetector.StaffSetReady = true
StaffDetector.StaffSetComplete = true
else
for userId in pairs(nextStaffUserIds) do
StaffDetector.StaffUserIds[userId] = true
end
StaffDetector.StaffSetReady = StaffDetector.StaffSetReady or next(StaffDetector.StaffUserIds) ~= nil
StaffDetector.StaffSetComplete = false
end
StaffDetector.StaffSetExpiresAt = os.clock()
+ (complete and StaffDetector.StaffSetLifetime or StaffDetector.FailureRetryDelay)
StaffDetector.StaffSetLoading = false
return StaffDetector.StaffSetReady
end

function StaffDetector.IsModFriend(player)
if not player or player == SDLP then return false end
local cached = StaffDetector.FriendCache[player.UserId]
if cached and cached.ExpiresAt > os.clock() then
return cached.Value, cached.StaffFriendName
end
if not StaffDetector.EnsureStaffUserIds() then
return nil
end
local friendsOk, friendPages = pcall(sdPlayers.GetFriendsAsync, sdPlayers, player.UserId)
if not friendsOk or not friendPages then
return nil
end
local matchedName = nil
local pageCount = 0
local complete = true
repeat
pageCount = pageCount + 1
local pageOk, friends = pcall(friendPages.GetCurrentPage, friendPages)
if not pageOk or type(friends) ~= "table" then
complete = false
break
end
for _, friend in ipairs(friends) do
local friendUserId = type(friend) == "table" and tonumber(friend.Id or friend.UserId or friend.id) or nil
if friendUserId and StaffDetector.StaffUserIds[friendUserId] then
matchedName = friend.Username or friend.DisplayName or tostring(friendUserId)
break
end
end
if matchedName or friendPages.IsFinished then
break
end
if pageCount >= StaffDetector.MaxFriendPages then
complete = false
break
end
local advanceOk = pcall(friendPages.AdvanceToNextPageAsync, friendPages)
if not advanceOk then
complete = false
break
end
until false
if not matchedName and (not complete or not StaffDetector.StaffSetComplete) then
return nil
end
local isModFriend = matchedName ~= nil
StaffDetector.FriendCache[player.UserId] = {
Value = isModFriend,
StaffFriendName = matchedName,
ExpiresAt = os.clock() + StaffDetector.FriendCacheLifetime,
}
return isModFriend, matchedName
end

function StaffDetector.NotifyKind(kind, names)
if not (config.staff.enabled and config.staff.actionNotify) then
StaffDetector.DestroyNotification(kind)
return
end
table.sort(names)
if #names == 0 then
StaffDetector.DestroyNotification(kind)
return
end
local title = kind == "Moderator" and "[Yuno] Moderator Detected" or "[Yuno] Mod Friend Detected"
local signature = kind .. "|" .. table.concat(names, ",")
local now = os.clock()
local last = StaffDetector.NotifyThrottle[signature]
if last and (now - last) < 90 then
return
end
for sig in pairs(StaffDetector.NotifyThrottle) do
if not sig:find(kind .. "|", 1, true) then
StaffDetector.NotifyThrottle[sig] = nil
end
end
StaffDetector.NotifyThrottle[signature] = now
StaffDetector.Notify(title, "In server: " .. table.concat(names, ", "))
end

function StaffDetector.UpdateNotifications()
for _, kind in ipairs({ "Moderator", "ModFriend" }) do
local names = {}
for _, detection in pairs(StaffDetector.Detections) do
if detection.Kind == kind and detection.Player and detection.Player.Parent == sdPlayers then
local name = detection.Player.DisplayName or detection.Player.Name
if kind == "ModFriend" and detection.StaffFriendName then
name = string.format("%s (friend of %s)", name, detection.StaffFriendName)
end
table.insert(names, name)
end
end
StaffDetector.NotifyKind(kind, names)
end
end

function StaffDetector.DestroyNotification(kind)
local notification = StaffDetector.Notifications[kind]
StaffDetector.Notifications[kind] = nil
end

function StaffDetector.RunActions(detection)
if not config.staff.enabled or not detection or not detection.Player then return end
local kind = detection.Kind
local actionPrefix = string.format("%s:%d:", kind, detection.Player.UserId)
if config.staff.actionKick then
local actionKey = actionPrefix .. "Kick"
if not StaffDetector.ActionCache[actionKey] then
StaffDetector.ActionCache[actionKey] = true
pcall(function()
SDLP:Kick(string.format(
"Staff Detector: %s detected (%s).",
detection.Player.Name,
kind == "Moderator" and "moderator" or "moderator friend"
))
end)
end
return
end
if config.staff.actionLobby and game.PlaceId ~= StaffDetector.LobbyPlaceId then
local actionKey = actionPrefix .. "ToLobby"
if not StaffDetector.ActionCache[actionKey] and os.clock() >= StaffDetector.EscapeCooldownUntil then
StaffDetector.EscapeCooldownUntil = os.clock() + 5
local ok = pcall(function()
sdTeleportService:Teleport(StaffDetector.LobbyPlaceId, SDLP)
end)
if ok then
StaffDetector.ActionCache[actionKey] = true
end
end
end
end

function StaffDetector.QueueClassification(player, delaySeconds)
if not config.staff.enabled or not player or player == SDLP then return end
local userId = player.UserId
local retryGeneration = (StaffDetector.RetryGeneration[userId] or 0) + 1
StaffDetector.RetryGeneration[userId] = retryGeneration
local detectorGeneration = StaffDetector.Generation
task.delay(delaySeconds or 0, function()
if not config.staff.enabled
or detectorGeneration ~= StaffDetector.Generation
or retryGeneration ~= StaffDetector.RetryGeneration[userId]
or player.Parent ~= sdPlayers
or StaffDetector.ClassificationInFlight[userId] then
return
end
StaffDetector.ClassificationInFlight[userId] = true
local isStaff = StaffDetector.IsStaff(player)
local detection = nil
local unavailable = isStaff == nil
if isStaff == true then
detection = { Player = player, Kind = "Moderator" }
elseif isStaff == false then
local isModFriend, staffFriendName = StaffDetector.IsModFriend(player)
unavailable = isModFriend == nil
if isModFriend == true then
detection = { Player = player, Kind = "ModFriend", StaffFriendName = staffFriendName }
end
end
StaffDetector.ClassificationInFlight[userId] = nil
if not config.staff.enabled
or detectorGeneration ~= StaffDetector.Generation
or retryGeneration ~= StaffDetector.RetryGeneration[userId]
or player.Parent ~= sdPlayers then
return
end
StaffDetector.Detections[userId] = detection
StaffDetector.UpdateNotifications()
if detection then
StaffDetector.RunActions(detection)
elseif unavailable then
StaffDetector.QueueClassification(player, StaffDetector.FailureRetryDelay)
end
end)
end

function StaffDetector.Refresh()
if not config.staff.enabled then
return
end
local activeUserIds = {}
for _, player in ipairs(sdPlayers:GetPlayers()) do
if player ~= SDLP then
activeUserIds[player.UserId] = true
StaffDetector.QueueClassification(player, 0)
end
end
for userId in pairs(StaffDetector.Detections) do
if not activeUserIds[userId] then
StaffDetector.Detections[userId] = nil
end
end
StaffDetector.UpdateNotifications()
end

function StaffDetector.Start()
if StaffDetector.Enabled then return end
StaffDetector.Enabled = true
StaffDetector.Generation = StaffDetector.Generation + 1
StaffDetector.Connections.PlayerAdded = sdPlayers.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
StaffDetector.QueueClassification(player, 0)
end)
StaffDetector.Connections.PlayerRemoving = sdPlayers.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
local userId = player.UserId
StaffDetector.RoleCache[userId] = nil
StaffDetector.FriendCache[userId] = nil
StaffDetector.Detections[userId] = nil
StaffDetector.ClassificationInFlight[userId] = nil
StaffDetector.RetryGeneration[userId] = nil
for actionKey in pairs(StaffDetector.ActionCache) do
if actionKey:find(":" .. tostring(userId) .. ":", 1, true) then
StaffDetector.ActionCache[actionKey] = nil
end
end
end)
StaffDetector.Refresh()
end

function StaffDetector.Stop()
StaffDetector.Enabled = false
StaffDetector.Generation = StaffDetector.Generation + 1
for key, connection in pairs(StaffDetector.Connections) do
pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
StaffDetector.Connections[key] = nil
end
table.clear(StaffDetector.Detections)
table.clear(StaffDetector.ActionCache)
table.clear(StaffDetector.ClassificationInFlight)
table.clear(StaffDetector.RetryGeneration)
end

do
local token = { dead = false }
yunoRegisterLive(function() token.dead = true end)
task.spawn(function()
while not token.dead do
pcall(function() StaffDetector.Refresh() end)
task.wait(10)
end
end)
end
end

getgenv().initYunoStaffDetector()

getgenv().initYunoWeaponMods = function()
local wmPlayers = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
local WMLP = wmPlayers.LocalPlayer
local WeaponMods = {
GunModule = nil,
MeleeModule = nil,
GunbladeModule = nil,
OriginalGunStartShooting = nil,
OriginalGunStartReloading = nil,
OriginalGunRecoil = nil,
OriginalGunGetAimSpeed = nil,
OriginalGunEquip = nil,
OriginalMeleeStartShooting = nil,
OriginalGunbladeStartShooting = nil,
}
getgenv().YunoWeaponMods = WeaponMods

local function resolveItemType(name)
local playerScripts = WMLP:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
local modulesFolder = playerScripts and playerScripts:FindFirstChild("Modules") or nil
local itemTypes = modulesFolder and modulesFolder:FindFirstChild("ItemTypes") or nil
local script = itemTypes and itemTypes:FindFirstChild(name) or nil
if not script then
return nil
end
local ok, result = pcall(require, script)
if ok then
return result
end
return nil
end

local function fireRateScale()
if not config.weapons.fireRateEnabled then
return 1
end
local boost = math.clamp(tonumber(config.weapons.fireRateBoost) or 85, 0, 85)
return 1 / (1 + (boost / 100))
end

local function resolveModItem(item)
if type(item) == "table" and type(item.Name) == "string" and item.Name ~= "" then
return item
end
for _, m in ipairs(getgc(true)) do
if type(m) == "table" and rawget(m, "LocalFighter") ~= nil then
local fighter = rawget(m, "LocalFighter")
local equipped = fighter and rawget(fighter, "EquippedItem") or nil
if type(equipped) == "table" and type(equipped.Name) == "string" then
return equipped
end
break
end
end
return nil
end

local function scaleField(info, field, scale, originals)
if type(info[field]) == "number" then
originals[field] = info[field]
info[field] = math.max(0.001, originals[field] * scale)
end
end

local function restoreFields(info, originals)
for field, value in pairs(originals) do
info[field] = value
end
end

function WeaponMods.EnsureHooks()
if not (config.weapons.fireRateEnabled or config.weapons.reloadEnabled
or config.weapons.noRecoilEnabled or config.weapons.adsEnabled
or config.weapons.equipEnabled) then
return false
end

if not WeaponMods.GunModule then
WeaponMods.GunModule = resolveItemType("Gun")
end
if not WeaponMods.MeleeModule then
WeaponMods.MeleeModule = resolveItemType("Melee")
end
if not WeaponMods.GunbladeModule then
local playerScripts = WMLP:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
local modulesFolder = playerScripts and playerScripts:FindFirstChild("Modules") or nil
local items = modulesFolder and modulesFolder:FindFirstChild("Items") or nil
local gbScript = items and items:FindFirstChild("Gunblade") or nil
if gbScript then
local ok, result = pcall(require, gbScript)
if ok then
WeaponMods.GunbladeModule = result
end
end
end

local gunModule = WeaponMods.GunModule
if gunModule then
if type(gunModule.StartShooting) == "function" and not WeaponMods.OriginalGunStartShooting then
local originalStartShooting = gunModule.StartShooting
WeaponMods.OriginalGunStartShooting = originalStartShooting
gunModule.StartShooting = function(self, ...)
local item = resolveModItem(self)
if not item then
return nil
end
local info = item and item.Info or nil
local originals = {}
if config.weapons.fireRateEnabled and info then
local scale = fireRateScale()
scaleField(info, "ShootCooldown", scale, originals)
scaleField(info, "ShootBurstCooldown", scale, originals)
scaleField(info, "QuickShotCooldown", scale, originals)
end
local results = table.pack(pcall(originalStartShooting, item, ...))
restoreFields(info, originals)
if not results[1] then
return nil
end
return table.unpack(results, 2, results.n)
end
end

if type(gunModule.StartReloading) == "function" and not WeaponMods.OriginalGunStartReloading then
local originalStartReloading = gunModule.StartReloading
WeaponMods.OriginalGunStartReloading = originalStartReloading
gunModule.StartReloading = function(self, forceReload, reloadEnum, animationEnum, segmentedCount)
if not config.weapons.reloadEnabled then
return originalStartReloading(self, forceReload, reloadEnum, animationEnum, segmentedCount)
end
local boost = math.clamp(tonumber(config.weapons.reloadBoost) or 10, 0, 20)
if boost <= 0 then
return originalStartReloading(self, forceReload, reloadEnum, animationEnum, segmentedCount)
end
local reloadDurationScale = 1 - (boost / 100)
local info = self and self.Info or nil
if not info then
return originalStartReloading(self, forceReload, reloadEnum, animationEnum, segmentedCount)
end
local reloadKey = "Reload"
local currentAmmo = nil
pcall(function()
local data = rawget(self, "Data")
currentAmmo = data and data.Ammo or nil
end)
if type(currentAmmo) == "number" and currentAmmo <= 0 and info.HasEmptyReload then
reloadKey = "EmptyReload"
end
local timingFields
if info.ReloadType == "Regular" then
timingFields = { reloadKey .. "Length", reloadKey .. "ActionTimestamp" }
elseif info.ReloadType == "Segmented" then
timingFields = {
reloadKey .. "StartLength", reloadKey .. "StartActionTimestamp",
reloadKey .. "SegmentLength", reloadKey .. "SegmentActionTimestamp",
reloadKey .. "FinishLength", reloadKey .. "FinishActionTimestamp",
}
else
return originalStartReloading(self, forceReload, reloadEnum, animationEnum, segmentedCount)
end
local originals = {}
for _, fieldName in ipairs(timingFields) do
local fieldValue = info[fieldName]
if type(fieldValue) == "number" then
originals[fieldName] = fieldValue
info[fieldName] = math.max(0.05, fieldValue * reloadDurationScale)
end
end
local results = table.pack(pcall(originalStartReloading, self, forceReload, reloadEnum, animationEnum, segmentedCount))
for fieldName, fieldValue in pairs(originals) do
info[fieldName] = fieldValue
end
if not results[1] then
return nil
end
return table.unpack(results, 2, results.n)
end
end

if type(gunModule._Recoil) == "function" and not WeaponMods.OriginalGunRecoil then
local originalRecoil = gunModule._Recoil
WeaponMods.OriginalGunRecoil = originalRecoil
gunModule._Recoil = function(self, multiplier)
if config.weapons.noRecoilEnabled and type(multiplier) == "number" then
local reduction = math.clamp(tonumber(config.weapons.recoilReduction) or 100, 0, 100)
local reducedMultiplier = multiplier * (1 - (reduction / 100))
if reducedMultiplier <= 0.001 then
return
end
return originalRecoil(self, reducedMultiplier)
end
return originalRecoil(self, multiplier)
end
end

if type(gunModule.GetAimSpeed) == "function" and not WeaponMods.OriginalGunGetAimSpeed then
local originalGetAimSpeed = gunModule.GetAimSpeed
WeaponMods.OriginalGunGetAimSpeed = originalGetAimSpeed
gunModule.GetAimSpeed = function(self)
local originalAimSpeed = originalGetAimSpeed(self)
if config.weapons.adsEnabled and type(originalAimSpeed) == "number" then
local boost = math.clamp(tonumber(config.weapons.adsBoost) or 85, 0, 85)
return math.max(1, originalAimSpeed * (1 + (boost / 100)))
end
return originalAimSpeed
end
end

if type(gunModule.Equip) == "function" and not WeaponMods.OriginalGunEquip then
local originalEquip = gunModule.Equip
WeaponMods.OriginalGunEquip = originalEquip
gunModule.Equip = function(self, ...)
local results = table.pack(originalEquip(self, ...))
if config.weapons.equipEnabled then
local boost = math.clamp(tonumber(config.weapons.equipBoost) or 100, 0, 80)
local equipSpeedScale = 1 / (1 + (boost / 100))
local now = tick()
local remaining = type(self._equip_cooldown) == "number" and math.max(self._equip_cooldown - now, 0) or 0
self._equip_cooldown = now + (remaining * equipSpeedScale)
local viewModel = self and self.ViewModel or nil
if viewModel then
task.delay(remaining * equipSpeedScale, function()
pcall(function()
if viewModel.StopAnimation then
viewModel:StopAnimation("Equip")
viewModel:StopAnimation("EquipEmpty")
end
end)
end)
end
end
return table.unpack(results, 1, results.n)
end
end
end

local meleeModule = WeaponMods.MeleeModule
if meleeModule and type(meleeModule.StartShooting) == "function" and not WeaponMods.OriginalMeleeStartShooting then
local originalMeleeShoot = meleeModule.StartShooting
WeaponMods.OriginalMeleeStartShooting = originalMeleeShoot
meleeModule.StartShooting = function(self, ...)
local item = resolveModItem(self)
if not item then
return nil
end
local info = item and item.Info or nil
local originals = {}
if config.weapons.fireRateEnabled and info then
scaleField(info, "AttackCooldown", fireRateScale(), originals)
end
local results = table.pack(pcall(originalMeleeShoot, item, ...))
restoreFields(info, originals)
if not results[1] then
return nil
end
return table.unpack(results, 2, results.n)
end
end

local gunbladeModule = WeaponMods.GunbladeModule
if gunbladeModule and type(gunbladeModule.StartShooting) == "function" and not WeaponMods.OriginalGunbladeStartShooting then
local originalGbShoot = gunbladeModule.StartShooting
WeaponMods.OriginalGunbladeStartShooting = originalGbShoot
gunbladeModule.StartShooting = function(self, ...)
local item = resolveModItem(self)
if not item then
return nil
end
local info = item and item.Info or nil
local originals = {}
local isBladeMode = false
pcall(function()
isBladeMode = item.Get and item:Get("Mode") == "Blade"
end)
if config.weapons.fireRateEnabled and info and isBladeMode then
scaleField(info, "BladeCooldown", fireRateScale(), originals)
end
local results = table.pack(pcall(originalGbShoot, item, ...))
restoreFields(info, originals)
if not results[1] then
return nil
end
return table.unpack(results, 2, results.n)
end
end

return true
end

do
local token = { dead = false }
yunoRegisterLive(function() token.dead = true end)
task.spawn(function()
while not token.dead do
pcall(function() WeaponMods.EnsureHooks() end)
task.wait(3)
end
end)
end
end

getgenv().initYunoWeaponMods()

local function _initRagebotTab()
local function startsync()
if config.rage.engine == "Void Engine" then return end
if config.state.csyncactive then return end
if config.target.immune then return end
if not config.target.character or not valid(config.target.character) then return end
eqSlot()
config.state.csyncactive = true
config.orbit.active = true
pcall(function() savedFallenPartsDestroyHeight = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight end)
pcall(function() workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0/0 end)
pcall(function() setfflag("DFIntS2PhysicsSenderRate", "120") end)
pcall(function() setfflag("DFIntAssemblyHistoryBufferSize", "1048576") end)
pcall(function() setfflag("DFIntAssemblyHistorySkipSize", "0") end)
if not config.orbit.savedpos then
if localfighter and localfighter.Entity and localfighter.Entity.RootPart then
config.orbit.savedpos = localfighter.Entity.RootPart.CFrame
end
end
local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if myRoot then
desync = RootDesync.new(myRoot)
end
config.orbit.connection = runsvc.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
if not config.state.csyncactive then return end
if config.target.immune then stopsync() return end
if not config.target.character or not valid(config.target.character) then stopsync() return end
if not localfighter or not localfighter.Entity or not localfighter.Entity.RootPart then stopsync() return end
local rp = localfighter.Entity.RootPart
if not desync or desync._rootPart ~= rp then
desync = RootDesync.new(rp)
end
local weapon = getweapon()
local checksling = isSling(weapon)
if reloadHide(checksling) then return end
if config.voidspam.enabled and not config.target.immune then
    tickVoidSpam()
    if config.voidspam.phase == "hide" then
        local myChar = player and player.Character
        local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local basePos = (root and root.Position) or (myRoot and myRoot.Position) or Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
        local randomAngle = math.random() * math.pi * 2
        local randomDist = math.random(15, 35)
        config.orbit.serverpos = basePos + Vector3.new(math.cos(randomAngle) * randomDist, -45, math.sin(randomAngle) * randomDist)
        desync:SetServerCFrame(CFrame.new(config.orbit.serverpos))
        desync:HeartbeatUpdate()
        return
    end
end
local root = config.target.character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not root then stopsync() return end
local now = os.clock()
local lf = modules.fighter and modules.fighter.LocalFighter
local equipped = lf and lf.EquippedItem
local weaponName = equipped and tostring(equipped:Get("Name") or ""):lower() or ""
local isKnife = weaponName:find("knife", 1, true) ~= nil or weaponName:find("melee", 1, true) ~= nil
if config.state.outofammo and weapon and not isInfiniteWeapon() then
    local myChar = player and player.Character
    local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local basePos = (root and root.Position) or (myRoot and myRoot.Position) or Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
    local randomAngle = math.random() * math.pi * 2
    local randomDist = math.random(15, 35)
    config.orbit.serverpos = basePos + Vector3.new(math.cos(randomAngle) * randomDist, -45, math.sin(randomAngle) * randomDist)
    desync:SetServerCFrame(CFrame.new(config.orbit.serverpos))
    desync:HeartbeatUpdate()
    return
end
if ragePerf.delayActive then
return
end

    local targetCFrame
    if checksling then
        targetCFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position)
    else
        local mode = config.target.attackPosition or "Adaptive Dynamic"
        local safeDist = tonumber(config.target.safeMeleeDist) or 6
        local look = root.CFrame.LookVector
        local right = root.CFrame.RightVector
        local tVel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
        local lead = tVel * 0.12
        local userOffset = (right * (tonumber(config.target.offsetX) or 0)) + Vector3.new(0, tonumber(config.target.offsetY) or 0, 0) + (look * (tonumber(config.target.offsetZ) or 0))

        if mode == "Custom Offset" then
            local customPos = root.Position + userOffset + lead
            targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(customPos, root.Position)
        elseif config.target.attackCustomEnabled then
            local frontOffset = config.target.customFront or 0
            local sideOffset = config.target.customSide or 0
            local vertOffset = config.target.customHeight or 2
            local targetPos = root.Position + (look * frontOffset) + (right * sideOffset) + Vector3.new(0, vertOffset, 0) + lead + userOffset
            local radius = config.target.customRadius or 0
            if radius > 0 then
                config.orbit.angle = (config.orbit.angle or 0) + ((config.orbit.orbitSpeed or 5000) * dt * 0.001)
                local orbitOffset = Vector3.new(
                    math.cos(config.orbit.angle) * radius,
                    0,
                    math.sin(config.orbit.angle) * radius
                )
                targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetPos + orbitOffset, root.Position)
            else
                targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetPos, root.Position)
            end
        elseif mode == "Adaptive Dynamic" or mode == "default" then
            if isKnife then
                local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                local headPos = (head and head.Position) or (root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.9, 0))
                local attackPos = headPos + Vector3.new(0, 0.45, 0) + lead + userOffset
                targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(attackPos, headPos - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
            elseif weaponName:find("shotgun") or weaponName:find("spray") or weaponName:find("exogun") then
                local sideJitter = math.sin(tick() * 8) * 3.5
                local flankPos = root.Position - (look * math.clamp(safeDist * 0.7, 3, 6)) + (right * sideJitter) + Vector3.new(0, 1.8, 0) + lead + userOffset
                targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(flankPos, root.Position)
            elseif weaponName:find("sniper") or weaponName:find("bow") then
                local snipePos = root.Position - (look * 7.5) + Vector3.new(0, 9, 0) + lead + userOffset
                targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(snipePos, root.Position)
            else
                local flankPos = root.Position - (look * safeDist) + (right * (math.sin(tick() * 6) * 2.5)) + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0) + lead + userOffset
                targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(flankPos, root.Position)
            end
        elseif mode == "Inside Target" or mode == "Inside" or mode == "zero" then
            local insidePos = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.4, 0) + lead + userOffset
            targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(insidePos, root.Position + look)
        elseif mode == "Below Target" or mode == "Under Void" or mode == "under" then
            local underPos = root.Position - Vector3.new(0, config.target.underOffset or 4.5, 0) + lead + userOffset
            targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(underPos, root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0))
        elseif mode == "Above Target" or mode == "High Vantage" then
            local highPos = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 6.5, 0) + lead + userOffset
            targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(highPos, root.Position)
        elseif mode == "Behind Target" or mode == "Dynamic Behind" or mode == "Backstab Lock" then
            local behindDist = isKnife and 2.5 or 3.8
            local behindPos = root.Position - (look * behindDist) + Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0) + lead + userOffset
            targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(behindPos, root.Position + look)
        elseif mode == "Bait Target" or mode == "Orbit" or mode == "Speed Orbit" or mode == "Adaptive Orbit" then
            local oscSide = math.sin(tick() * 9) * 4.5
            local oscDist = 3.2 + math.cos(tick() * 7) * 1.8
            local oscVert = 1.5 + math.sin(tick() * 5) * 1.2
            local baitPos = root.Position - (look * oscDist) + (right * oscSide) + Vector3.new(0, oscVert, 0) + lead + userOffset
            targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(baitPos, root.Position)
        else
            local targetPos = root.Position - (look * safeDist) + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0) + lead + userOffset
            targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetPos, root.Position)
        end
    end

if config.rage.defensiveCFrame and not checksling then
targetCFrame = computeDefensiveCFrame(targetCFrame, nil, root)
end
config.orbit.serverpos = targetCFrame.Position
if not getgenv().YunoVoidActive then
local distance = (targetCFrame.Position - rp.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
if distance < 2000 then
desync:SetServerCFrame(targetCFrame)
end
end
desync:HeartbeatUpdate()
end)
end

local function autoshoot()
if config.rage.engine == "Void Engine" then return end
if not isSling(getweapon()) and config.voidspam.enabled and config.voidspam.phase == "hide" then return end
if not config.target.enabled or not config.target.character or not config.target.autoshoot then return end
if config.target.immune then return end
if not ragePerf.canShoot then return end
local weapon = getweapon()
local checksling = isSling(weapon)
eqSlot()
local lf = modules.fighter and modules.fighter.LocalFighter
if not lf or not lf.EquippedItem then return end
if not isInfiniteWeapon() then
if config.state.reloading or config.state.outofammo then return end
if not canuse() then return end
end
if not shootLock:ShouldFire(1 / 60, config.rage.shootFrames) then return end
local targetChar = config.target.character
if not valid(targetChar) then return end
local hitPart = hitpartfromname(targetChar, config.target.hitpart)
if not hitPart then return end
local myRoot = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local shootPos
if config.state.csyncactive and config.orbit.serverpos then
shootPos = config.orbit.serverpos
else
shootPos = myRoot and myRoot.Position or Vector3.new()
end
local targetPos = predict(hitPart, shootPos)
local aimCFrame = CFrame.new(shootPos, targetPos)
local attackData = {
[utf8.char(0)] = modules.utility:EncodeCFrame(aimCFrame),
[utf8.char(1)] = modules.utility:EncodeCFrame(aimCFrame),
[utf8.char(2)] = hitPart,
[utf8.char(3)] = modules.utility:EncodeCFrame(CFrame.new(0.43, 0.25, 0.42)),
}
local equipped = lf.EquippedItem
if not equipped or not equipped:Get("ObjectID") then return end
local objId = equipped:Get("ObjectID")
local weaponName = tostring(equipped:Get("Name") or ""):lower()
local isKnife = weaponName:find("knife", 1, true) ~= nil or weaponName:find("melee", 1, true) ~= nil
local now = os.clock()
if isKnife and config.backstab.enabled then
if now < config.backstab.attackCooldownUntil then return end
local data = {
[utf8.char(1)] = attackData,
}
ragePerf.lastAttackTick = tick()
ragePerf.lastFireTime = tick()
pcall(function()
replicatedstorage.Remotes.Replication.Fighter.UseItem:FireServer(
objId,
modules.enums:ToEnum("StartAiming"),
data,
nil
)
end)
recordBackstab()
else
local data = {
[utf8.char(1)] = attackData,
}
ragePerf.lastAttackTick = tick()
ragePerf.lastFireTime = tick()
local rageMode = config.target.rageMode or "Adaptive Dominance"
local attempts = math.floor(tonumber(config.target.shootAttempts) or 1)
if rageMode == "HvH Hyper-Aggressive" then
attempts = math.max(attempts, 3)
end
for _ = 1, attempts do
pcall(function()
replicatedstorage.Remotes.Replication.Fighter.UseItem:FireServer(
objId,
modules.enums:ToEnum("StartShooting"),
data,
nil
)
end)
end
end
hideShot()
end

local oldcamupdate = modules.camcontrol.Update
modules.camcontrol.Update = function(...)
if getgenv().YunoVoidActive then
return oldcamupdate(...)
elseif config.state.csyncactive and desync then
desync:RestoreClientCFrame()
end
local results = {oldcamupdate(...)}
return unpack(results)
end

local immuneList = {}
local onImmune = {}
local onVulnerable = {}

local function bindimmune(callback)
table.insert(onImmune, callback)
end

local function bindvulnerable(callback)
table.insert(onVulnerable, callback)
end

local _lastImmuneScan = 0
runsvc.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - _lastImmuneScan < 0.15 then return end
    _lastImmuneScan = now

    for _, plr in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr == player then continue end
        local char = plr.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local immune = root and root:FindFirstChild("Attachment") ~= nil
        if immune and not immuneList[plr.Name] then
            immuneList[plr.Name] = true
            for _, cb in ipairs(onImmune) do cb(plr) end
        elseif not immune and immuneList[plr.Name] then
            immuneList[plr.Name] = nil
            for _, cb in ipairs(onVulnerable) do cb(plr) end
        end
    end
end)

bindimmune(function(plr)
if config.target.lastplayer == plr then
config.target.immune = true
config.voidspam.phase = nil
clrVoidSnap()
indicator.Color = Color3.fromRGB(180, 0, 255)
stopsync()
end
end)

bindvulnerable(function(plr)
if config.target.lastplayer == plr then
config.target.immune = false
indicator.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
if config.target.enabled and config.target.character and valid(config.target.character) then
startsync()
end
end
end)

local function cleartarget()
config.target.enabled = false
stopsync()
config.target.character = nil
getgenv().YunoCurrentRageLock = nil
config.target.lastchar = nil
config.target.lastplayer = nil
config.target.manualkey = false
config.target.immune = false
if ragePerf.delayTask then task.cancel(ragePerf.delayTask) ragePerf.delayTask = nil end
ragePerf.delayActive = false
ragePerf.canShoot = false
ragePerf.lastFireTime = 0
getgenv()._rageCanShoot = false
getgenv()._rageDelayActive = false
indicator.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
end

local function trackKill(targetPlayer)
if not targetPlayer then return end
config.kills.count = config.kills.count + 1
config.kills.streak = config.kills.streak + 1
config.kills.lastKillAt = tick()
end

local function settarget(char)
if not char then return end
clrVoidSnap()
vhState.active = false
local targetplr = players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char)
config.target.enabled = true
config.target.character = char
getgenv().YunoCurrentRageLock = char
config.target.lastchar = char
config.target.lastplayer = targetplr
config.target.immune = false
ragePerf.targetAcquiredAt = tick()
ragePerf.canShoot = false
getgenv()._rageCanShoot = false
if ragePerf.delayTask then task.cancel(ragePerf.delayTask) end
ragePerf.delayActive = true
getgenv()._rageDelayActive = true
indicator.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local isImmune = root and root:FindFirstChild("Attachment")
if isImmune then
config.target.immune = true
indicator.Color = Color3.fromRGB(180, 0, 255)
end
if not isImmune then
eqSlot()
startsync()
end
ragePerf.delayTask = task.spawn(function()
task.wait(ragePerf.shootDelay)
if not config.target.enabled or config.target.character ~= char then return end
ragePerf.delayActive = false
getgenv()._rageDelayActive = false
ragePerf.lastFireTime = tick() - ragePerf.shootCooldown
ragePerf.canShoot = true
getgenv()._rageCanShoot = true
end)
end

local sling = {
enabled = false,
connections = {}
}

local function checksling()
return isSling(getweapon())
end

local function nearplr()
local root = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not root then return nil end
local found, best = nil, math.huge
for _, p in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
if p == player or not p.Character then continue end
local head = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HitboxHead") or p.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
if not head then continue end
local d = (head.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
if d < best then
found = head
best = d
end
end
return found
end

local function slingshotTP()
if sling.connections.touch then return end
sling.connections.touch = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
if d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Model") then
if d.Name == "Slingshot" or d.Name == "CoreProjectile" or d.Name == "OuterProjectile" then
task.spawn(function()
task.wait(0.06)
local part = d:IsA("BasePart") and d or d:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
if not part then return end
part.CanTouch = true
for i = 1, 60 do
if not part.Parent or not part:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then break end
local target = nearplr()
if target and target:IsA("BasePart")
and target.Parent
and target:IsDescendantOf(workspace)
then
pcall(firetouchinterest, target, part, 0)
pcall(firetouchinterest, target, part, 1)
end
task.wait()
end
end)
end
end
end)
end

local function stopslingTP()
if sling.connections.touch then
sling.connections.touch:Disconnect()
sling.connections.touch = nil
end
end

local function targetpos()
if not config.target.character then return nil end
local root = config.target.character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
return root and root.Position
end

local function updatesling()
local slingon = checksling()
local hastarget = config.target.enabled and config.target.character ~= nil
if slingon then
if not sling.enabled then
sling.enabled = true
slingshotTP()
end
if getgenv().yunoSetVoidPos then
getgenv().yunoSetVoidPos(true)
end
vhState.active = hastarget
if hastarget and not config.state.csyncactive then
startsync()
elseif not hastarget and config.state.csyncactive and not config.target.enabled then
stopsync()
end
else
if sling.enabled then
sling.enabled = false
stopslingTP()
end
if Toggles.AntiAimUnderground and not Toggles.AntiAimUnderground.Value then
if getgenv().yunoSetVoidPos then
getgenv().yunoSetVoidPos(false)
end
end
vhState.active = false
end
end

local _lastFFlagVal = nil
local function fflag()
    local hastarget = config.target.enabled and config.target.character and valid(config.target.character)
    local slingon = checksling()
    local desired = (hastarget and slingon) and "999999" or "1"
    if _lastFFlagVal ~= desired then
        _lastFFlagVal = desired
        pcall(function()
            if setfflag then
                setfflag("TargetTimeDelayFacctorTenths", desired)
            end
        end)
    end
end

local targetHudAnimLast = tick()
runsvc.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
local inLobby = getgenv().yunoInLobbyZone and getgenv().yunoInLobbyZone()
local hudNeeded = config and config.visualizer and config.visualizer.enabled
or (config and config.ragestatus and config.ragestatus.enabled)
or (config and config.hitNotifications and config.hitNotifications.enabled)
or (config and config.target and config.target.enabled and config.target.character and config.prediction and config.prediction.enabled)
if inLobby and not hudNeeded then
return
end
if shouldSuppressGameplayOverlays() then
if tracerline then tracerline.Visible = false end
if traceroutline then traceroutline.Visible = false end
if manipStatus then manipStatus.Visible = false end
if ragebot.hideRageStatus then
ragebot.hideRageStatus()
end
if ragebot.updateHitNotifications then
ragebot.updateHitNotifications(0)
end
return
end
local now = tick()
local hudDt = math.clamp(now - targetHudAnimLast, 0, 0.05)
targetHudAnimLast = now
if config and config.ragestatus and config.ragestatus.enabled and ragebot.drawStatus then
ragebot.drawStatus()
elseif ragebot.hideRageStatus then
ragebot.hideRageStatus()
end
if ragebot.updateHitNotifications then
ragebot.updateHitNotifications(hudDt)
end
if silentAim and manipulation and silentAim.enabled and manipulation.enabled then
local myChar = player.Character
local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if myRoot then
local closest = closestplayerinfov(silentAim.fovRadius)
if closest then
local part = hitpartfromname(closest, silentAim.hitPart)
if part then
local shootPos = myRoot.Position
local targetPos = part.Position
local wallRayParams = RaycastParams.new()
wallRayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {myChar, closest}
wallRayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
local wallHit = workspace:Raycast(shootPos, targetPos - shootPos, wallRayParams)
if wallHit then
local manipPos = manipulationCalcPoint(shootPos, targetPos, closest)
if manipPos then
local screenPos, onScreen = workspace.CurrentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
if onScreen then
manipStatus.Text = "manipulate"
manipStatus.Position = Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y - 30)
manipStatus.Visible = true
else
manipStatus.Visible = false
end
else
manipStatus.Visible = false
end
else
manipStatus.Visible = false
end
else
manipStatus.Visible = false
end
else
manipStatus.Visible = false
end
else
manipStatus.Visible = false
end
else
manipStatus.Visible = false
end
    -- Target Dot Visualizer
    if getgenv().YunoTargetDot and config.target.enabled and config.target.character then
        local tChar = config.target.character
        local tPart = getTargetPart(tChar, config.target.hitpart) or (tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("Head"))
        if tPart and tPart:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
            local sp, onScreen = workspace.CurrentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(tPart.Position)
            if onScreen then
                indicator.Position = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y)
                indicator.Color = getgenv().YunoTargetDotColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                indicator.Radius = 6
                indicator.Filled = true
                indicator.Visible = true
                indicatoroutline.Position = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y)
                indicatoroutline.Radius = 7
                indicatoroutline.Visible = true
            else
                indicator.Visible = false
                indicatoroutline.Visible = false
            end
        else
            indicator.Visible = false
            indicatoroutline.Visible = false
        end
    else
        if indicator and not (config and config.visualizer and config.visualizer.enabled) then
            indicator.Visible = false
            indicatoroutline.Visible = false
        end
    end

    -- Target Highlight (Chams)
    if getgenv().YunoTargetHighlight and config.target.enabled and config.target.character then
        local tChar = config.target.character
        if tChar and tChar:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
            local hl = tChar:FindFirstChild("YunoRageTargetHL")
            if not hl then
                hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
                hl.Name = "YunoRageTargetHL"
                hl.FillTransparency = 0.5
                hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
                hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
                hl.Parent = tChar
            end
            hl.OutlineColor = getgenv().YunoTargetHighlightColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 80)
            hl.FillColor = getgenv().YunoTargetHighlightColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 80)
            hl.Enabled = true
        end
    end

    -- Desync Server Hitbox Ghost Visualizer
    if getgenv().YunoDesyncGhost and config.orbit.serverpos then
        local sPos = config.orbit.serverpos
        local sp, onScreen = workspace.CurrentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(sPos)
        if onScreen and (sPos - workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude < 1000 then
            if not getgenv()._YunoGhostCircle then
                getgenv()._YunoGhostCircle = Drawing.new("Circle")
                getgenv()._YunoGhostCircle.Thickness = 2
                getgenv()._YunoGhostCircle.Filled = false
                getgenv()._YunoGhostCircle.NumSides = 24
            end
            local gc = getgenv()._YunoGhostCircle
            gc.Position = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y)
            gc.Radius = 14
            gc.Color = getgenv().YunoDesyncGhostColor or Color3.fromRGB(140, 90, 255)
            gc.Visible = true
        else
            if getgenv()._YunoGhostCircle then
                getgenv()._YunoGhostCircle.Visible = false
            end
        end
    else
        if getgenv()._YunoGhostCircle then
            getgenv()._YunoGhostCircle.Visible = false
        end
    end

    if config.target.enabled and config.target.character and config.prediction.enabled then
        updatevel()
    end
end)

local function isSpawnSafe()
local lp = player
if not lp then return false end
for _, key in ipairs({"InSpawn", "InLobby", "IsSpectating"}) do
local v = lp:GetAttribute(key)
if v == true or v == 1 or v == "true" then
return true
end
local char = lp.Character
if char then
local cv = char:GetAttribute(key)
if cv == true or cv == 1 or cv == "true" then
return true
end
end
end
return false
end

runsvc.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
pcall(fflag)
pcall(updatesling)
pcall(tickAmmo)
if isSpawnSafe() then
if config.target.enabled then cleartarget() end
return
end
if config.target.auto and not config.target.manualkey and rageOn() then
local myChar = player.Character
local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if config.target.safeHP > 0 and myHum and myHum.Health < config.target.safeHP then
if config.target.enabled then cleartarget() end
return
end
local current, _, melee = getammo()
local hasammo = current == nil or current > 0 or melee
if hasammo and not config.state.reloading then
if not config.target.enabled or not config.target.character or not valid(config.target.character) then
local newtarget = nearest()
if newtarget then
if config.target.enabled then stopsync() end
settarget(newtarget)
else
end
end
end
end
if config.target.enabled and config.target.character then
if not valid(config.target.character) then
if config.target.lastplayer then
trackKill(config.target.lastplayer)
end
if config.target.autoSwitch then
local newtarget = nearest()
if newtarget then
cleartarget()
settarget(newtarget)
return
end
end
cleartarget()
return
end
if not ragePerf.delayActive then
eqSlot()
end
if config.target.autoshoot and not config.target.immune then
autoshoot()
end
end
end)

ragebot.config = config
ragebot.nearest = nearest
ragebot.settarget = settarget
ragebot.cleartarget = cleartarget
ragebot.vhState = vhState
ragebot.ragePerf = ragePerf
ragebot.getammo = getammo
ragebot.muzzlepos = muzzlepos
ragebot.valid = valid
ragebot.player = player
ragebot.eqSlot = eqSlot
ragebot.refreshAtk = refreshAtk
ragebot.rageOn = rageOn
ragebot.startsync = startsync
ragebot.stopsync = stopsync

do
local ragebot = getgenv()._YunoRagebot or ragebot or {}
local config = ragebot.config or {}
if not config.ragestatus then
    config.ragestatus = {
        enabled = false,
        mode = "static",
        textSize = 13,
        color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        staticOffsetX = 0,
        staticOffsetY = 0,
    }
end
ragebot.config = config
local ragePerf = ragebot.ragePerf
local getammo = ragebot.getammo
local muzzlepos = ragebot.muzzlepos
local valid = ragebot.valid
local player = ragebot.player
local vhState = ragebot.vhState
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

local function mkStatusLbl()
    local ok, d = pcall(function() return Drawing.new("Text") end)
    local raw = (ok and d) or { Visible = false, Text = "", Position = Vector2.zero }
    local label = (getgenv().yunoRegisterText and getgenv().yunoRegisterText(raw)) or raw
    pcall(function()
        label.Size = config.ragestatus.textSize or 13
        label.Font = 2
        label.Outline = true
        label.Center = true
        label.Visible = false
        label.Color = config.ragestatus.color
        label.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    end)
    return label
end

local rageStatusLine1 = mkStatusLbl()
local rageStatusLine2 = mkStatusLbl()

local function styleStatusLbl(label)
label.Size = config.ragestatus.textSize or 13
label.Font = 2
label.Outline = true
label.Color = config.ragestatus.color
pcall(function()
label.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
end)
end

local function statusAnchor()
if config.ragestatus.mode == "muzzle" then
local mp = muzzlepos and muzzlepos()
if mp then
local wts = getgenv().yunoProjectWorld or worldToScreen
local screenPos, onScreen = wts(mp, camera)
if onScreen and screenPos then
return Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y)
end
end
end
local cam = camera or workspace.CurrentCamera
if not cam then
return Vector2.zero
end
return (cam.ViewportSize / 2) + Vector2.new(
config.ragestatus.staticOffsetX or 0,
config.ragestatus.staticOffsetY or 0
)
end

local function rageActive()
return config.target.enabled or config.target.auto or config.voidspam.enabled
end

local function killNm()
if config.target.lastplayer then
return config.target.lastplayer.Name
end
if config.target.character then
local tp = game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayerFromCharacter(config.target.character)
if tp then
return tp.Name
end
end
return "target"
end

local function inVoid()
    if vhState.active then return true end
    if config.voidspam.enabled and config.voidspam.phase == "hide" then
        return true
    end
    if config.state.reloading and config.ragestatus.hideOnReload ~= false then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

local function isKilling()
    if not rageActive() then
        return false
    end
    if config.state.reloading or inVoid() then
        return false
    end
    if config.target.immune then
        return false
    end
    if not config.target.enabled or not config.target.character or not valid(config.target.character) then
        return false
    end
    if config.state.csyncactive then
        return true
    end
    if config.voidspam.enabled and config.voidspam.phase == "shoot" then
        return true
    end
    if tick() - (ragePerf.lastAttackTick or 0) < math.max(ragePerf.shootCooldown, 0.05) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

local function hudAmmo()
if isInfiniteWeapon() then return "∞" end
local cur, maxAmmo, melee = getammo()
if cur == nil then return "∞" end
if melee then return "melee" end
return string.format("%d/%d", math.floor(cur or 0), math.max(math.floor(maxAmmo or 0), 0))
end

local function fmtVsTime(minT, maxT)
local a = clampVs(minT)
local b = clampVs(maxT)
if b < a then
a, b = b, a
end
if math.abs(a - b) < 0.05 then
return string.format("%.1fs", a)
end
return string.format("%.1f-%.1fs", a, b)
end

local function vsDetail()
if not config.voidspam.enabled then
return ""
end
local vs = config.voidspam
return string.format(
"void %s atk · %s hide",
fmtVsTime(vs.shoot_min, vs.shoot_max),
fmtVsTime(vs.hide_min, vs.hide_max)
)
end

local function getEnemyHealth()
if not config.target.character then return "?" end
local hum = config.target.character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hum then return "?" end
return string.format("%.0f", hum.Health)
end

local function rageLines()
if isSpawnSafe() then
return "ragebot: idle"
end
local now = tick()
if isKilling() then
if not ragePerf.killStartAt then
ragePerf.killStartAt = now
end
else
ragePerf.killStartAt = nil
end
if config.state.reloading then
return "ragebot: reloading..."
end
if inVoid() then
return "ragebot: void"
end
if isKilling() then
local name = killNm()
return string.format("ragebot: killing %s...", name)
end
if not config.target.enabled then
local lp = player
if lp then
for _, key in ipairs(DUEL_STATE_ATTRS) do
local v = lp:GetAttribute(key)
if v == true or v == 1 or v == "true" then
return "ragebot: void"
end
local char = lp.Character
if char then
local cv = char:GetAttribute(key)
if cv == true or cv == 1 or cv == "true" then
return "ragebot: void"
end
end
end
end
end
return "ragebot: idle"
end

local function drawStatus()
local isMenuOpen = (Library and Library.Toggled) or (getgenv().Library and getgenv().Library.Toggled)
if not config.ragestatus.enabled or shouldSuppressGameplayOverlays() or isMenuOpen then
rageStatusLine1.Visible = false
rageStatusLine2.Visible = false
return
end
local anchor = statusAnchor()
local gap = config.ragestatus.lineGap or 14
if ragePerf.lastRageColor ~= config.ragestatus.color then
ragePerf.lastRageColor = config.ragestatus.color
styleStatusLbl(rageStatusLine1)
styleStatusLbl(rageStatusLine2)
end
local mainLine = rageLines()
local cur, maxAmmo = getammo()
local ammoStr = cur ~= nil and string.format("%d/%d", math.floor(cur), math.max(math.floor(maxAmmo or 0), 0)) or "?/?"
local hp = getEnemyHealth()
local killStr = config.ragestatus.showKills and string.format(" [%d]", config.kills.count) or ""
local line2Text = string.format("%s/%s%s", ammoStr, hp, killStr)
local lineCount = 2
local topOffset = -(lineCount - 1) * (gap / 2)
if mainLine ~= ragePerf.lastRageStatusText then
ragePerf.lastRageStatusText = mainLine
rageStatusLine1.Text = mainLine
end
rageStatusLine1.Position = anchor + Vector2.new(0, topOffset)
rageStatusLine1.Visible = config.ragestatus.enabled and not isMenuOpen
local nextY = topOffset + gap
if line2Text ~= ragePerf.lastRageDetailText then
ragePerf.lastRageDetailText = line2Text
rageStatusLine2.Text = line2Text
end
rageStatusLine2.Position = anchor + Vector2.new(0, nextY)
rageStatusLine2.Visible = config.ragestatus.enabled and not isMenuOpen
end

ragebot.drawStatus = drawStatus
ragebot.hideRageStatus = function()
rageStatusLine1.Visible = false
rageStatusLine2.Visible = false
end
ragebot.clrVoidSnap = clrVoidSnap
end

        auto = false,

        shootAttempts = 1,

        rageMasterOn = false,

if not config.rage then
    config.rage = {

        evasion = "Random",

        show = true,

if not config.ragestatus then
    config.ragestatus = {
        enabled = false,

    }
end
ragebot.config = config

local startsync = ragebot.startsync or function() end
local stopsync = ragebot.stopsync or function() end

local function togglekey()
if not rageOn() then return end
if config.target.auto then return end
if config.target.enabled and config.target.character then
cleartarget()
else
local target = nearest()
if target then
config.target.manualkey = true
settarget(target)
end
end
end

local combatTab = (Tabs and (Tabs.Combat or Tabs.Rage)) or (getgenv().Tabs and (getgenv().Tabs.Combat or getgenv().Tabs.Rage))

else

end
end

-- Unified single weapon selector for Void Engine
rageui.ragebotBox:AddDropdown("RageWeaponPick", {
    Text = "weapon",
    Default = config.target.weaponPick or "primary",
    Values = { "primary", "secondary", "melee", "knife" },
    Callback = function(val)
        config.target.weaponPick = val
        local vLow = tostring(val):lower()
        local isKnifeSelected = (vLow == "knife" or vLow == "melee")
        config.rage.weaponsEnabled = {
            Primary = (vLow == "primary"),
            Secondary = (vLow == "secondary"),
            Melee = isKnifeSelected,
        }
        if isKnifeSelected then
            config.backstab.enabled = true
            getgenv().KnifeBackstabLock = true
            getgenv().KnifeAbilitySpam = true
        end
        if config.state.csyncactive and ragebot.refreshAtk then
            ragebot.refreshAtk()
        end
        if ragebot.eqSlot then
            ragebot.eqSlot()
        end
    end,
})

    Default = false,
    Tooltip = "rapidly phases into void between shots to evade incoming fire",
    Callback = function(val)
        config.voidspam.enabled = val
        if val then
            if config.target.immune then
                config.voidspam.phase = nil
                return
            end
            config.voidspam.lastswitch = tick()
            config.voidspam.phase = "shoot"
            config.voidspam.currentduration = randVs(config.voidspam.shoot_min or 0.5, config.voidspam.shoot_max or 1.5)
        else
            config.voidspam.phase = nil
            if ragebot.clrVoidSnap then
                ragebot.clrVoidSnap()
            end
        end
    end
})
