        ibCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        ibCorner.Parent = itemBtn

        itemBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            itemBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(44, 34, 70)
            itemBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        end)
        itemBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            itemBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 22, 44)
            itemBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        end)
        itemBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if Library and Library.PlaySound then Library:PlaySound('click') end
            onClick()
        end)
        return itemBtn
    end

    -- Floating Target Weapon Dropdown Picker
    local targetWeaponDropdown = Instance.new("Frame")
    targetWeaponDropdown.Name = "TargetWeaponDropdown"
    targetWeaponDropdown.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 0, 200)
    targetWeaponDropdown.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 4, 0, 356)
    targetWeaponDropdown.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 17, 36)
    targetWeaponDropdown.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    targetWeaponDropdown.BorderSizePixel = 0
    targetWeaponDropdown.ZIndex = 98
    targetWeaponDropdown.Visible = false
    targetWeaponDropdown.Parent = studioMain
    local twdCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    twdCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    twdCorner.Parent = targetWeaponDropdown
    local twdStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    twdStroke.Color = (Library and Library.AccentColor) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 145, 255)
    twdStroke.Transparency = 0.3
    twdStroke.Thickness = 1.2
    twdStroke.Parent = targetWeaponDropdown

    local twdTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    twdTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 22)
    twdTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 4)
    twdTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    twdTitle.Text = "SELECT TARGET WEAPON"
    twdTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    twdTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    twdTitle.TextSize = 10
    twdTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    twdTitle.ZIndex = 99
    twdTitle.Parent = targetWeaponDropdown

    local twdScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    twdScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -30)
    twdScroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 26)
    twdScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    twdScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
    twdScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    twdScroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = (Library and Library.AccentColor) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 145, 255)
    twdScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    twdScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    twdScroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    twdScroll.ZIndex = 99
    twdScroll.Parent = targetWeaponDropdown
    local twdLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    twdLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
    twdLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    twdLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
    twdLayout.Parent = twdScroll
    local twdPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    twdPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2)
    twdPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4)
    twdPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 2)
    twdPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4)
    twdPadding.Parent = twdScroll

    for _, targetW in ipairs(WEAPONS) do
        local twItem = Instance.new("TextButton")
        twItem.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
        twItem.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(26, 20, 42)
        twItem.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
        twItem.Text = ""
        twItem.AutoButtonColor = false
        twItem.ZIndex = 100
        twItem.Parent = twdScroll
        local twiCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        twiCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        twiCorner.Parent = twItem

        local twiIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        twiIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 18)
        twiIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -9)
        twiIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        twiIcon.Image = WEAPON_IMAGES[targetW] or "rbxassetid://17160682738"
        twiIcon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        twiIcon.ZIndex = 101
        twiIcon.Parent = twItem

        local twiLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        twiLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 1, 0)
        twiLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 0)
        twiLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        twiLabel.Text = targetW
        twiLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        twiLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        twiLabel.TextSize = 11
        twiLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        twiLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        twiLabel.ZIndex = 101
        twiLabel.Parent = twItem

        twItem.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            twItem.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(44, 34, 70)
            twiLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        end)
        twItem.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            twItem.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(26, 20, 42)
            twiLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        end)
        twItem.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if Library and Library.PlaySound then Library:PlaySound('click') end
            local curItem = ss.equipped and ss.equipped[currentWeapon] and ss.equipped[currentWeapon][currentCategory]
            if curItem and curItem.Name then
                SC.scEquip(targetW, currentCategory, curItem.Name, { seed = customSeed, inverted = customInverted })
                if updateAllStudioWeaponCards then updateAllStudioWeaponCards() end
                if Library and Library.Notify then Library:Notify(string.format("Applied %s to %s!", curItem.Name, targetW)) end
            else
                if Library and Library.Notify then Library:Notify('No ' .. currentCategory .. ' equipped on ' .. currentWeapon) end
            end
            targetWeaponDropdown.Visible = false
            optionsDropdown.Visible = false
            optionsBtn.Text = "options  ▼"
        end)
    end

    -- Options Items:
    createOptionItem("Apply to all weapons", function()
        local curItem = ss.equipped and ss.equipped[currentWeapon] and ss.equipped[currentWeapon][currentCategory]
        if curItem and curItem.Name then
            for _, w in ipairs(WEAPONS) do
                SC.scEquip(w, currentCategory, curItem.Name, { seed = customSeed, inverted = customInverted })
            end
            if updateAllStudioWeaponCards then updateAllStudioWeaponCards() end
            if Library and Library.Notify then Library:Notify('Applied ' .. curItem.Name .. ' to all weapons!') end
        else
            if Library and Library.Notify then Library:Notify('No ' .. currentCategory .. ' equipped on ' .. currentWeapon) end
        end
        optionsDropdown.Visible = false
        optionsBtn.Text = "options  ▼"
    end)

    createOptionItem("Apply to specific weapon...", function()
        targetWeaponDropdown.Visible = not targetWeaponDropdown.Visible
        if targetWeaponDropdown.Visible and dropdownMenu then
            dropdownMenu.Visible = false
            categoryBtn.Text = currentCategory .. "  ▼"
        end
    end)

    createOptionItem("Reset all to defaults", function()
        if ss.equipped then
            ss.equipped = {}
            if SC and SC.scSave then SC.scSave() end
            if SC and SC.scRefreshHeldWeapon then SC.scRefreshHeldWeapon() end
            if updateAllStudioWeaponCards then updateAllStudioWeaponCards() end
            if populateStudioCosmetics then populateStudioCosmetics() end
            if Library and Library.Notify then Library:Notify('Reset all cosmetics to defaults!') end
        end
        optionsDropdown.Visible = false
        targetWeaponDropdown.Visible = false
        optionsBtn.Text = "options  ▼"
    end)

    optionsBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        optionsBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(38, 28, 60)
        optStroke.Color = (Library and Library.AccentColor) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 145, 255)
    end)
    optionsBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not optionsDropdown.Visible then
            optionsBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(26, 20, 42)
            optStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(65, 52, 90)
        end
    end)
    optionsBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if Library and Library.PlaySound then Library:PlaySound('click') end
        optionsDropdown.Visible = not optionsDropdown.Visible
        optionsBtn.Text = optionsDropdown.Visible and "options  ▲" or "options  ▼"
        if not optionsDropdown.Visible then
            targetWeaponDropdown.Visible = false
        end
        if optionsDropdown.Visible and dropdownMenu then
            dropdownMenu.Visible = false
            categoryBtn.Text = currentCategory .. "  ▼"
        end
    end)

    local hintLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    hintLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 18)
    hintLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 594)
    hintLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    hintLabel.Text = "right click a weapon to toggle / clear cosmetics for it."
    hintLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 175, 205)
    hintLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    hintLabel.TextSize = 10
    hintLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    hintLabel.ZIndex = 12
    hintLabel.Parent = studioMain

    -- ================================================================
    -- RENDER WEAPON CARDS & COSMETICS
    -- ================================================================
    updateAllStudioWeaponCards = function()
        local wQuery = string.lower(weaponFilterBox.Text or "")
        for wName, card in pairs(weaponCards) do
            local isSel = (wName == currentWeapon)
            local img = getWeaponEquippedImage(wName)

            card.Icon.Image = img
            if isSel then
                card.Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 34, 72)
                card.Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
                card.Stroke.Color = (Library and Library.AccentColor) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 145, 255)
                card.Stroke.Transparency = 0.1
                card.Title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                card.SelectedIndicator.Visible = true
            else
                card.Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 17, 34)
                card.Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
                card.Stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 42, 72)
                card.Stroke.Transparency = 0.6
                card.Title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 195, 220)
                card.SelectedIndicator.Visible = false
            end

            if wQuery == "" then
                card.Frame.Visible = true
            else
                card.Frame.Visible = (string.find(string.lower(wName), wQuery, 1, true) ~= nil)
            end
        end
    end

        local function filterWeaponsFast()
            local wQuery = string.lower(weaponFilterBox.Text or "")
            for wName, card in pairs(weaponCards) do
                if wQuery == "" then
                    card.Frame.Visible = true
                else
                    card.Frame.Visible = (string.find(string.lower(wName), wQuery, 1, true) ~= nil)
                end
            end
        end

        weaponFilterBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            filterWeaponsFast()
        end)

        populateStudioCosmetics = function()
            for _, ch in ipairs(botScroll:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.Name ~= "UIGridLayout" and ch.Name ~= "UIPadding" then
                    ch:Destroy()
                end
            end

            local cQuery = string.lower(cosmeticFilterBox.Text or "")
            local lib = getCosmeticLib()
            local items = {}
            if currentCategory == "Skin" then
                items = getSkinsForWeapon(currentWeapon)
                if #items == 0 then items = getKeysByType("Skin") end
            elseif currentCategory == "Wrap" then
                items = getKeysByType("Wrap")
            elseif currentCategory == "Charm" then
                items = getKeysByType("Charm")
            elseif currentCategory == "Finisher" then
                items = getKeysByType("Finisher")
            elseif currentCategory == "Emote" then
                items = getKeysByType("Emote")
            end

            table.sort(items, function(a, b)
                local da = (lib and lib.Cosmetics and lib.Cosmetics[a] and lib.Cosmetics[a].DisplayName) or a
                local db = (lib and lib.Cosmetics and lib.Cosmetics[b] and lib.Cosmetics[b].DisplayName) or b
                return tostring(da):lower() < tostring(db):lower()
            end)

            -- "Random" cosmetic card at the start
            local randomCard = Instance.new("TextButton")
            randomCard.Name = "CosmeticCard_Random"
            randomCard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 126, 0, 96)
            randomCard.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 18, 40)
            randomCard.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
            randomCard.BorderSizePixel = 0
            randomCard.Text = ""
            randomCard.AutoButtonColor = false
            randomCard.ZIndex = 12
            randomCard.Parent = botScroll
            local rcCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            rcCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
            rcCorner.Parent = randomCard
            local rcStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            rcStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(56, 48, 78)
            rcStroke.Transparency = 0.6
            rcStroke.Parent = randomCard
            local rcIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            rcIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 48)
            rcIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -24, 0, 12)
            rcIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            rcIcon.Image = "rbxassetid://10709791437"
            rcIcon.ImageColor3 = (Library and Library.AccentColor) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 145, 255)
            rcIcon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
            rcIcon.ZIndex = 13
            rcIcon.Parent = randomCard
            local rcTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            rcTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 20)
            rcTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -24)
            rcTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            rcTitle.Text = "Random"
            rcTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 235, 255)
            rcTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            rcTitle.TextSize = 11
            rcTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            rcTitle.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            rcTitle.ZIndex = 13
            rcTitle.Parent = randomCard
            randomCard.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if Library and Library.PlaySound then Library:PlaySound('click') end
                if #items > 0 then
                    local pick = items[math.random(1, #items)]
                    SC.scEquip(currentWeapon, currentCategory, pick, { seed = customSeed, inverted = customInverted })
                    updateAllStudioWeaponCards()
                    populateStudioCosmetics()
                end
            end)

            for _, key in ipairs(items) do
                local data = lib and lib.Cosmetics and lib.Cosmetics[key]
                local displayName = data and data.DisplayName or key
                local itemImg = data and data.Image and data.Image ~= "" and data.Image or (WEAPON_IMAGES[currentWeapon] or "")
                local isEquipped = (ss.equipped and ss.equipped[currentWeapon] and ss.equipped[currentWeapon][currentCategory] and ss.equipped[currentWeapon][currentCategory].Name == key)

                local card = Instance.new("TextButton")
                card.Name = "CosmeticCard_" .. key
                card.Size = UDim2.new(0, 126, 0, 96)
                card.BackgroundColor3 = isEquipped and Color3.fromRGB(48, 36, 76) or Color3.fromRGB(22, 17, 34)
                card.BackgroundTransparency = isEquipped and 0.15 or 0.55
                card.BorderSizePixel = 0
                card.Text = ""
                card.AutoButtonColor = false
                card.ZIndex = 12
                card.Parent = botScroll

                local cCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
                cCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
                cCorner.Parent = card

                local cStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
                cStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
                cStroke.Color = isEquipped and ((Library and Library.AccentColor) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 145, 255)) or Color3.fromRGB(56, 48, 78)
                cStroke.Transparency = isEquipped and 0.1 or 0.6
                cStroke.Thickness = 1
                cStroke.Parent = card

                local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 74, 0, 60)
                icon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -37, 0, 6)
                icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                icon.Image = itemImg
                icon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
                icon.ZIndex = 13
                icon.Parent = card

                local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 20)
                title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -24)
                title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                title.Text = displayName
                title.TextColor3 = isEquipped and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(215, 210, 235)
                title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                title.TextSize = 11
                title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                title.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                title.ZIndex = 13
                title.Parent = card

                if cQuery ~= "" and not string.find(string.lower(displayName), cQuery, 1, true) and not string.find(string.lower(key), cQuery, 1, true) then
                    card.Visible = false
                end

                card.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                    if not isEquipped then
                        card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(36, 28, 56)
                        card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
                        cStroke.Color = (Library and Library.AccentColor) or Color3.fromRGB(160, 125, 235)
                        cStroke.Transparency = 0.4
                    end
                end)
                card.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                    if not isEquipped then
                        card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 17, 34)
                        card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
                        cStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(56, 48, 78)
                        cStroke.Transparency = 0.6
                    end
                end)

                card.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    if Library and Library.PlaySound then Library:PlaySound('click') end
                    if isEquipped then
                        if ss.equipped and ss.equipped[currentWeapon] then
                            ss.equipped[currentWeapon][currentCategory] = nil
                            if SC and SC.scSave then SC.scSave() end
                            if SC and SC.scRefreshHeldWeapon then SC.scRefreshHeldWeapon(currentWeapon) end
                        end
                        if Library and Library.Notify then Library:Notify(string.format("Unequipped %s", displayName)) end
                    else
                        if SC and SC.scEquip then
                            SC.scEquip(currentWeapon, currentCategory, key, { seed = customSeed, inverted = customInverted })
                        end
                        if Library and Library.Notify then Library:Notify(string.format("Equipped %s on %s!", displayName, currentWeapon)) end
                    end
                    updateAllStudioWeaponCards()
                    populateStudioCosmetics()
                end)
            end
        end

        local function filterCosmeticsFast()
            local cQuery = string.lower(cosmeticFilterBox.Text or "")
            for _, card in ipairs(botScroll:GetChildren()) do
                if card:IsA("GuiObject") and card.Name ~= "UIGridLayout" and card.Name ~= "UIPadding" then
                    if cQuery == "" then
                        card.Visible = true
                    else
                        local tLbl = card:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                        local txt = tLbl and string.lower(tLbl.Text) or ""
                        local cKey = string.lower(card.Name or "")
                        card.Visible = (string.find(txt, cQuery, 1, true) ~= nil or string.find(cKey, cQuery, 1, true) ~= nil)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        cosmeticFilterBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            filterCosmeticsFast()
        end)

    local function selectWeapon(wName)
        currentWeapon = wName
        updateAllStudioWeaponCards()
        populateStudioCosmetics()
    end

    for _, wName in ipairs(WEAPONS) do
        local card = Instance.new("TextButton")
        card.Size = UDim2.new(0, 126, 0, 96)
        card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 17, 34)
        card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
        card.BorderSizePixel = 0
        card.Text = ""
        card.AutoButtonColor = false
        card.ZIndex = 11
        card.Parent = topScroll

        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        corner.Parent = card

        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 42, 72)
        stroke.Transparency = 0.6
        stroke.Thickness = 1
        stroke.Parent = card

        local selIndicator = Instance.new("Frame")
        selIndicator.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8)
        selIndicator.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 6)
        selIndicator.BackgroundColor3 = (Library and Library.AccentColor) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 145, 255)
        selIndicator.BorderSizePixel = 0
        selIndicator.Visible = false
        selIndicator.ZIndex = 14
        selIndicator.Parent = card
        local siCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        siCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        siCorner.Parent = selIndicator

        local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 60)
        icon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -40, 0, 6)
        icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        icon.Image = WEAPON_IMAGES[wName] or "rbxassetid://17160682738"
        icon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        icon.ZIndex = 12
        icon.Parent = card

        local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 20)
        title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -24)
        title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        title.Text = wName
        title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        title.TextSize = 11
        title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        title.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        title.ZIndex = 12
        title.Parent = card

        card.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if wName ~= currentWeapon then
                card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(36, 28, 56)
                card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
                stroke.Color = (Library and Library.AccentColor) or Color3.fromRGB(160, 125, 235)
                stroke.Transparency = 0.4
            end
        end)
        card.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            if wName ~= currentWeapon then
                card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 17, 34)
                card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
                stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 42, 72)
                stroke.Transparency = 0.6
            end
        end)

        card.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if Library and Library.PlaySound then Library:PlaySound('click') end
            selectWeapon(wName)
        end)

        -- Right click to reset/clear cosmetics on this weapon
        card.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
            if Library and Library.PlaySound then Library:PlaySound('click') end
            if ss.equipped and ss.equipped[wName] then
                ss.equipped[wName] = {}
                if SC and SC.scSave then SC.scSave() end
                if SC and SC.scRefreshHeldWeapon then SC.scRefreshHeldWeapon(wName) end
                if Library and Library.Notify then Library:Notify("Cleared cosmetics on " .. wName) end
                updateAllStudioWeaponCards()
                populateStudioCosmetics()
            end
        end)

        weaponCards[wName] = {
            Frame = card,
            Stroke = stroke,
            Icon = icon,
            Title = title,
            SelectedIndicator = selIndicator,
        }
    end

    selectWeapon(currentWeapon)
end

local function openStudioGui()
    createStudioGui()
    if studioGui then
        studioGui.Enabled = not studioGui.Enabled
        if studioGui.Enabled then
            if updateAllStudioWeaponCards then updateAllStudioWeaponCards() end
            if populateStudioCosmetics then populateStudioCosmetics() end
        end
    end
end

if Library then
    Library.OnToggle = Library.OnToggle or {}
    table.insert(Library.OnToggle, function(state)
        if not state and studioGui then
            studioGui.Enabled = false
        end
    end)
end

-- Reworked Skins Tab Controls (Main Window)
local SkinsL = Tabs.Skins:AddLeftGroupbox('skin changer')
local SkinsR = Tabs.Skins:AddRightGroupbox('customization & batch tools')

SkinsL:AddButton('open skin changer', function()
openStudioGui()
end)

SkinsL:AddLabel('opens the full visual weapon, skin, wrap, charm & finisher studio window', true)

SkinsL:AddDivider()

SkinsL:AddButton('unlock all cosmetics', function()
local ok = SC.scUnlockAll and SC.scUnlockAll()
if ok then
Library:Notify('All skins, wraps, and cosmetics unlocked!')
else
Library:Notify('Cosmetics system ready!')
end
if updateAllStudioWeaponCards then updateAllStudioWeaponCards() end
if populateStudioCosmetics then populateStudioCosmetics() end
end)

SkinsL:AddButton('save cosmetics config', function()
if SC and SC.scSave then SC.scSave() end
Library:Notify('Cosmetics configuration saved!')
end):AddButton('load cosmetics config', function()
if SC and SC.scLoad then SC.scLoad() end
if updateAllStudioWeaponCards then updateAllStudioWeaponCards() end
if populateStudioCosmetics then populateStudioCosmetics() end
Library:Notify('Cosmetics configuration loaded!')
end)

SkinsL:AddDivider()

SkinsL:AddButton('clear current weapon cosmetics', function()
if ss.equipped and ss.equipped[currentWeapon] then
ss.equipped[currentWeapon] = {}
if updateAllStudioWeaponCards then updateAllStudioWeaponCards() end
if populateStudioCosmetics then populateStudioCosmetics() end
Library:Notify('Cleared cosmetics on ' .. currentWeapon)
end
end)

SkinsR:AddSlider('CustomSeedSlider', {
Text = 'pattern seed',
Default = 1,
Min = 1,
Max = 1000000,
Rounding = 0,
Compact = true,
Callback = function(val)
customSeed = math.floor(tonumber(val) or 1)
end
})

SkinsR:AddToggle('InvertedToggle', {
Text = 'inverted pattern',
Default = false,
Callback = function(val)
customInverted = val
end
})

SkinsR:AddDivider()

SkinsR:AddButton('apply current skin to ALL weapons', function()
local curSkin = ss.equipped and ss.equipped[currentWeapon] and ss.equipped[currentWeapon].Skin
if curSkin and curSkin.Name then
for _, w in ipairs(WEAPONS) do
SC.scEquip(w, "Skin", curSkin.Name, { seed = customSeed, inverted = customInverted })
end
if updateAllStudioWeaponCards then updateAllStudioWeaponCards() end
Library:Notify('Applied ' .. curSkin.Name .. ' to all weapons!')
end
end)

SkinsR:AddButton('apply current wrap to ALL weapons', function()
local curWrap = ss.equipped and ss.equipped[currentWeapon] and ss.equipped[currentWeapon].Wrap
if curWrap and curWrap.Name then
for _, w in ipairs(WEAPONS) do
SC.scEquip(w, "Wrap", curWrap.Name, { seed = customSeed, inverted = customInverted })
end
if updateAllStudioWeaponCards then updateAllStudioWeaponCards() end

end
end)

if ss.equipped then
ss.equipped = {}
if updateAllStudioWeaponCards then updateAllStudioWeaponCards() end
if populateStudioCosmetics then populateStudioCosmetics() end
end

local ConfigsGroup = Tabs['UI Settings']:AddRightGroupbox('configs')
local configBtnRefs = {}
local selectedConfigName = nil

local configScrollFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
configScrollFrame.Name = "ConfigListScroll"
configScrollFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 130)
configScrollFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
configScrollFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
configScrollFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 2
configScrollFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Library.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(140, 82, 255)
configScrollFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
configScrollFrame.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
configScrollFrame.Parent = ConfigsGroup.Container

local configListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
configListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
configListLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
configListLayout.Parent = configScrollFrame

local function refreshConfigButtons()
for _, ref in ipairs(configBtnRefs) do
pcall(ref.Destroy, ref)
end
configBtnRefs = {}
local list = {}
pcall(function() list = SaveManager:RefreshConfigList() end)

local currentAutoload = 'none'
pcall(function() currentAutoload = SaveManager:GetAutoloadConfig() end)

for _, name in ipairs(list) do
local isSelected = (selectedConfigName == name)
local isAutoload = (currentAutoload == name)

local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
btn.Name = "ConfigBtn_" .. name
btn.BackgroundColor3 = isSelected and (Library.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(140, 82, 255)) or (Library.MainColor or Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28))
btn.BackgroundTransparency = isSelected and 0.35 or 0.2
btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 24)
btn.Text = ""
btn.AutoButtonColor = false
btn.ZIndex = 15
btn.Active = true
btn.Parent = configScrollFrame

local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
corner.Parent = btn

local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
stroke.Color = isSelected and (Library.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(140, 82, 255)) or (Library.OutlineColor or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 55))
stroke.Transparency = isSelected and 0 or 0.35
stroke.Thickness = isSelected and 1.5 or 1
stroke.Parent = btn

local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
label.Name = "Label"
label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0)
label.Font = Library.Font or Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
label.TextSize = 11
label.TextColor3 = isSelected and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or (Library.FontColor or Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220))
label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
label.Text = isAutoload and (name .. " [AUTOLOAD]") or name
label.ZIndex = 16
label.Active = false
label.Parent = btn

local function OnSelect()
selectedConfigName = name
pcall(function()
if Options and Options.SaveManager_ConfigName then
Options.SaveManager_ConfigName:SetValue(name)
end
end)
refreshConfigButtons()
end

btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(OnSelect)
btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
OnSelect()
end
end)

btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
if selectedConfigName ~= name then
stroke.Color = Library.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(140, 82, 255)
stroke.Transparency = 0.1
end
end)
btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
if selectedConfigName ~= name then
stroke.Color = Library.OutlineColor or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 55)
stroke.Transparency = 0.35
end
end)

table.insert(configBtnRefs, btn)
end
end

local function nameOrWarn()
local n = selectedConfigName
if Options and Options.SaveManager_ConfigName and Options.SaveManager_ConfigName.Value ~= "" then
n = Options.SaveManager_ConfigName.Value
end
if not n or n:gsub(' ', '') == '' then
Library:Notify('enter or select a config name', 2)
return nil
end
return n
end

ConfigsGroup:AddButton('refresh list', refreshConfigButtons)

ConfigsGroup:AddInput('SaveManager_ConfigName', {
Text = 'config name',
Callback = function(val)
if val and val ~= "" then
selectedConfigName = val
end
end
})

ConfigsGroup:AddButton('load config', function()
local name = nameOrWarn() if not name then return end
local ok, err = pcall(function() return SaveManager:Load(name) end)
if ok then
Library:Notify(string.format('Loaded config %q', name))
else
Library:Notify('Failed to load config: ' .. tostring(err))
end
end):AddButton('create config', function()
local name = nameOrWarn() if not name then return end
local ok, err = pcall(function() return SaveManager:Save(name) end)
if ok then
Library:Notify(string.format('Created config %q', name))
selectedConfigName = name
refreshConfigButtons()
else
Library:Notify('Failed: ' .. tostring(err))
end
end)

ConfigsGroup:AddButton('overwrite config', function()
local name = nameOrWarn() if not name then return end
local ok, err = pcall(function() return SaveManager:Save(name) end)
if ok then
Library:Notify(string.format('Overwrote config %q', name))
refreshConfigButtons()
else
Library:Notify('Failed: ' .. tostring(err))
end
end):AddButton('delete config', function()
local name = nameOrWarn() if not name then return end
local ok, err = pcall(function() return SaveManager:Delete(name) end)
if ok then
Library:Notify(string.format('Deleted config %q', name))
if selectedConfigName == name then selectedConfigName = nil end
refreshConfigButtons()
else
Library:Notify('Failed: ' .. tostring(err))
end
end)

ConfigsGroup:AddDivider()

local autoloadName = 'none'
pcall(function() autoloadName = SaveManager:GetAutoloadConfig() end)
local autoloadLabel = ConfigsGroup:AddLabel("autoload: " .. autoloadName, true)

local function updateAutoloadLabel()
local current = 'none'
pcall(function() current = SaveManager:GetAutoloadConfig() end)
if autoloadLabel and autoloadLabel.SetText then
pcall(autoloadLabel.SetText, autoloadLabel, "autoload: " .. current)
end
refreshConfigButtons()
end

ConfigsGroup:AddButton('set as autoload', function()
local name = nameOrWarn() if not name then return end
local ok, err = pcall(function() return SaveManager:SaveAutoloadConfig(name) end)
if ok then
Library:Notify(string.format('Set %q as autoload config!', name))
updateAutoloadLabel()
else
Library:Notify('Failed to set autoload: ' .. tostring(err))
end
end):AddButton('reset autoload', function()
pcall(function() SaveManager:DeleteAutoLoadConfig() end)
Library:Notify('Autoload config reset to none')
updateAutoloadLabel()
end)

ConfigsGroup:AddDivider()

ConfigsGroup:AddToggle('AutoLoadToggle', {
Text = 'auto load on teleport / startup',
Default = false,
Tooltip = 'persists script and config across teleports and executor restarts',
Callback = function(val)
getgenv().YunoAutoExecFlag = val
if _G.Features then _G.Features.AutoLoadOnTeleport = val end
if val then
if getgenv().QueueYunoOnTeleport then getgenv().QueueYunoOnTeleport() end
Library:Notify("Auto-load armed across all teleports & lobbies!", 3)
else
pcall(function()
writefile("yuno/autoload.txt", "false")
pcall(delfile, "autoexec/yunoloader.lua")
end)
Library:Notify("Auto-load disabled", 2)
end
end
})

local autosaveConn = nil
ConfigsGroup:AddToggle('AutoSaveToggle', {
Text = 'autosave (every 60s)',
Default = false,
Callback = function(val)
if val then
if not autosaveConn then
autosaveConn = task.spawn(function()
while Toggles and Toggles.AutoSaveToggle and Toggles.AutoSaveToggle.Value do
task.wait(60)
if Toggles and Toggles.AutoSaveToggle and Toggles.AutoSaveToggle.Value then
pcall(function() SaveManager:Save('autosave') end)
end
end
autosaveConn = nil
end)
end
Library:Notify('Autosave enabled')
else
if autosaveConn then
task.cancel(autosaveConn)
autosaveConn = nil
end
Library:Notify('Autosave disabled')
end
end
})

local _lastNoFlashTick = 0
game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
local now = os.clock()
if now - _lastNoFlashTick < 0.5 then return end
_lastNoFlashTick = now
local LP = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local pg = LP and LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
if pg then
if _G.yunoNoFlash then
for _, g in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
if string.find(string.lower(g.Name), "flash") or string.find(string.lower(g.Name), "blind") then
pcall(function() g.Enabled = false end)
end
end
end
if _G.yunoNoVignette then
for _, g in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
if g:IsA("ImageLabel") and (string.find(string.lower(g.Name), "vignette") or string.find(string.lower(g.Name), "lowhealth") or string.find(string.lower(g.Name), "damageindicator")) then
g.Visible = false
end
end
end
end
end)

visualOptBox:AddToggle("UltraFPSBooster", {
Text = "ultra fps booster (max fps)",
Default = false,
Tooltip = "removes shadows, post-fx & terrain clutter to maximize raw FPS",
Callback = function(val)
pcall(function()
local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
if val then
lighting.GlobalShadows = false
lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
if terrain then
terrain.Decoration = false
terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
end
for _, v in ipairs(lighting:GetChildren()) do
if v:IsA("PostEffect") or v:IsA("BloomEffect") or v:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or v:IsA("BlurEffect") then
if v.Name ~= "YunoUiBlur" then
v.Enabled = false
end
end
end
-- setfpscap unrestricted
else
lighting.GlobalShadows = true
end
end)
end
})
end)

evaderBox:AddDropdown("ProtectedActionChoice", {
Text = "on protected detected",
Values = {"Notify Only", "Server Hop", "Auto Leave"},
Default = "Notify Only",
})
end)

local shootSoundDebounce = 0
local lastMapUpdate = 0
local lastHudUpdate = 0
local lastKnifeCheck = 0
local _staffCache = {}

task.spawn(function()
while true do
task.wait(4)
pcall(function()
if getgenv().StaffDetector or getgenv().ProtectedDetector then
for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if pl ~= LocalPlayer then
if _staffCache[pl.UserId] == nil then
local isStaff = false
local isProtected = false
local rank = 0
pcall(function() rank = pl:GetRankInGroup(game.CreatorId) end)
if rank >= 100 or pl.UserId == game.CreatorId then isStaff = true end
if pl:GetAttribute("IsStaff") or pl:GetAttribute("Admin") or pl:GetAttribute("Mod") then isStaff = true end
if pl:GetAttribute("Verified") or pl:GetAttribute("Creator") or pl:GetAttribute("Streamer") then isProtected = true end
_staffCache[pl.UserId] = { isStaff = isStaff, isProtected = isProtected, rank = rank }
end
local info = _staffCache[pl.UserId]
if info.isStaff and getgenv().StaffDetector then
local act = Options.StaffActionChoice and Options.StaffActionChoice.Value or "Notify Only"
Library:Notify(string.format("[STAFF DETECTED] %s (Rank %d)", pl.Name, info.rank), 6)
if act == "Server Hop" then
TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer)
elseif act == "Auto Leave" then
game:Shutdown()
end
elseif info.isProtected and getgenv().ProtectedDetector then
local act = Options.ProtectedActionChoice and Options.ProtectedActionChoice.Value or "Notify Only"
Library:Notify(string.format("[PROTECTED / CREATOR] %s detected", pl.Name), 5)
if act == "Server Hop" then
TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer)
elseif act == "Auto Leave" then
game:Shutdown()
end
end
end
end
end
end)
end
end)

Camera.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
if getgenv().RemoveGameDamageNumbers then
if child:IsA("BillboardGui") and (child.Name:find("Damage") or child.Name:find("Indicator")) then
child.Enabled = false
end
end
end)

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if (not Toggles or not Toggles.MiniMapToggle or not Toggles.MiniMapToggle.Value) and (not Toggles or not Toggles.TargetHUDToggle or not Toggles.TargetHUDToggle.Value) then return end
local now = tick()
local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not myRoot or not myHum or myHum.Health <= 0 then return end

if Toggles.MiniMapToggle and Toggles.MiniMapToggle.Value and (now - lastMapUpdate) > 0.033 then
lastMapUpdate = now
local myPos = myRoot.Position
local myYaw = math.atan2(-Camera.CFrame.LookVector.X, -Camera.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
local entities = {}
local curTgt = getgenv().YunoCurrentRageLock or curtarget

for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if pl ~= LocalPlayer and pl.Character then
local hrp = pl.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hum = pl.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
local isTeam = (pl.Team and LocalPlayer.Team and pl.Team == LocalPlayer.Team)
table.insert(entities, {
Pos = hrp.Position,
Player = pl,
IsTeammate = isTeam,
IsTarget = (pl == curTgt or pl.Character == curTgt),
})
end
end
end
local zoomVal = Options.MiniMapZoomSlider and Options.MiniMapZoomSlider.Value or 180
Library:UpdateMiniMap(myPos, myYaw, entities, curTgt, zoomVal)
end

if Toggles.TargetHUDToggle and Toggles.TargetHUDToggle.Value and (now - lastHudUpdate) > 0.033 then
lastHudUpdate = now
local tgtChar = getgenv().YunoCurrentRageLock or curtarget
if typeof(tgtChar) == "Instance" and not tgtChar:IsA("Model") then
tgtChar = tgtChar.Parent
end
if tgtChar and tgtChar:IsA("Model") then
local hum = tgtChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local hrp = tgtChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local pl = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(tgtChar)
if hum and hum.Health > 0 and hrp then
local dist = (hrp.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
local wepName = "Primary"
for _, child in ipairs(tgtChar:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("Tool") or child.Name:find("Gun") or child.Name:find("Rifle") or child.Name:find("Knife") then
wepName = child.Name
break
end
end
Library:SetTargetHUD({
Target = tgtChar,
UserId = pl and pl.UserId or 0,
Name = pl and pl.DisplayName or tgtChar.Name,
Health = hum.Health,
MaxHealth = hum.MaxHealth,
Shield = tgtChar:GetAttribute("Shield") or 0,
MaxShield = 50,
Distance = dist,
Weapon = wepName,
})
else
Library:SetTargetHUD(nil)
end
else
Library:SetTargetHUD(nil)
end
end

local isKnifeActive = (config and config.target and (config.target.weaponPick == "knife" or config.target.weaponPick == "melee")) or getgenv().AlwaysBackstab or getgenv().KnifeHitboxExpander
if isKnifeActive and (now - lastKnifeCheck) > 0.016 then
lastKnifeCheck = now
local equippedTool = myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
local isKnife = equippedTool and (equippedTool.Name:lower():find("knife") or equippedTool.Name:lower():find("dagger") or equippedTool.Name:lower():find("katana") or equippedTool.Name:lower():find("axe") or equippedTool.Name:lower():find("melee") or equippedTool.Name:lower():find("scythe") or equippedTool.Name:lower():find("fist"))

if isKnife then
local maxReach = 8 * (getgenv().KnifeReach or 2.5)
local bestTarget, bestDist = nil, math.huge

for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if pl ~= LocalPlayer and pl.Character and (not pl.Team or pl.Team ~= LocalPlayer.Team) then
local eRoot = pl.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local eHum = pl.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if eRoot and eHum and eHum.Health > 0 then
local dist = (eRoot.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
if dist <= maxReach and dist < bestDist then
bestDist = dist
bestTarget = pl
end
end
end
end

if bestTarget and bestTarget.Character then
local eRoot = bestTarget.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if eRoot then
local targetLook = eRoot.CFrame.LookVector

-- Teleport / Angle behind player for guaranteed lethal backstab
local backstabPos = eRoot.Position - (targetLook * math.clamp(bestDist, 1.6, 2.5)) + Vector3.new(0, 0.4, 0)
myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(backstabPos, eRoot.Position)

-- Rapidly spam knife ability & slashes
if (now - lastShotTime) >= 0.02 then
lastShotTime = now
pcall(function()
if equippedTool.Activate then equippedTool:Activate() end
local rem = equippedTool:FindFirstChildOfClass("RemoteEvent") or equippedTool:FindFirstChildOfClass("RemoteFunction")
if rem then
pcall(function() rem:FireServer("StartAiming") end)
pcall(function() rem:FireServer("StartShooting") end)
end
end)
end
end
end
end
end
end)

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and getgenv().CustomShootSoundEnabled then
if (tick() - shootSoundDebounce) > 0.08 then
shootSoundDebounce = tick()
local soundType = Options.CustomShootSoundChoice and Options.CustomShootSoundChoice.Value or "Rust"
local soundId = nil
if soundType == "Custom" then
soundId = Options.CustomShootSoundId and Options.CustomShootSoundId.Value
else
soundId = soundsassets and soundsassets[soundType] or "rbxassetid://1255040462"
end
if soundId and soundId ~= "" then
local s = Instance.new("Sound")
s.SoundId = tostring(soundId)
s.Volume = Options.ShootSoundVolume and Options.ShootSoundVolume.Value or 0.6
s.PlaybackSpeed = Options.ShootSoundPitch and Options.ShootSoundPitch.Value or 1
s.Parent = ScreenGui
s:Play()
s.Ended:Connect(function() s:Destroy() end)
end
end
end
end)
end

getgenv().YunoConfigBusy = true

task.defer(function()
task.wait(0.35)
pcall(function()
local opt = Options and Options.tgbtgbbktbkmb
if opt and setfpscap then
local v = tonumber(opt.Value) or 240
if v <= 0 or v >= 9999 then
setfpscap(240)
else
setfpscap(math.floor(math.clamp(v, 30, 1000)))
end
end
end)
task.wait(0.3)
pcall(function()
if (_G.Features and _G.Features.SilentLoad) or (Toggles and Toggles.silent_load and Toggles.silent_load.Value) then
if Library and Library.Toggled then
Library:Toggle()
end
end
end)
pcall(function()
    if Tabs and Tabs.Rage and Tabs.Rage.ShowTab then
        Tabs.Rage:ShowTab()
    elseif Window and Window.Tabs and Window.Tabs[1] and Window.Tabs[1].ShowTab then
        Window.Tabs[1]:ShowTab()
    end
end)
getgenv().YunoConfigBusy = false
end)
end
_initThemeAndSave()
